/// @note Call write() from one producer and read() from one consumer.
/// @note Prepare and clear with both sides stopped. Query fill from either owning side.
class FrameRing
{
  public:
    /// @brief Allocate storage and clear the queue on success.
    void prepare(std::size_t channels, std::size_t capacity)
    {
        // Buffer's float storage limit also bounds the cursor arithmetic below.
        storage_.resize(channels, capacity);
        capacity_ = capacity;
        clear();
    }

    void clear() noexcept
    {
        read_.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        write_.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    }

    std::size_t capacity() const noexcept { return capacity_; }

    std::size_t available() const noexcept
    {
        const auto read = read_.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
        return queued(write_.load(std::memory_order_acquire), read);
    }

    /// @brief Sample queue occupancy from outside the producer and consumer.
    /// @return False if the independently sampled cursors yield an out-of-range result.
    /// @pre Serialize with prepare().
    bool estimate(std::size_t &frames) const noexcept
    {
        frames = available();
        return frames <= capacity_;
    }

    /// @pre The ring is prepared and the input matches its layout.
    std::size_t write(IFBlock input) noexcept
    {
        assert(capacity_ && input.valid() && input.channels() == storage_.block(0).channels());
        const auto write = write_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        const auto frames = std::min(input.frames(), capacity_ - queued(write, read_.load(std::memory_order_acquire)));
        if (frames)
        {
            copy(input, storage_.block(capacity_), write, frames, true);
            write_.store(advance(write, frames), std::memory_order_release);
        }
        return frames;
    }

    /// @pre The ring is prepared and the output matches its layout.
    std::size_t read(OFBlock output) noexcept
    {
        assert(capacity_ && output.valid() && output.channels() == storage_.block(0).channels());
        const auto read = read_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        const auto frames = std::min(output.frames(), queued(write_.load(std::memory_order_acquire), read));
        if (frames)
        {
            copy(storage_.block(capacity_), output, read, frames, false);
            read_.store(advance(read, frames), std::memory_order_release);
        }
        return frames;
    }

  private:
    void copy(IFBlock input, OFBlock output, std::size_t cursor, std::size_t frames, bool writing) noexcept
    {
        // Cursors wrap at twice the capacity.
        const auto offset = cursor < capacity_ ? cursor : cursor - capacity_;
        const auto first = std::min(frames, capacity_ - offset);
        const auto second = frames - first;
        const auto src_first = writing ? std::size_t{0} : offset;
        const auto dst_first = writing ? offset : std::size_t{0};
        const auto src_second = writing ? first : std::size_t{0};
        const auto dst_second = writing ? std::size_t{0} : first;
        for (std::size_t ch = 0; ch < input.channels(); ++ch)
        {
            const auto *src = input.channel(ch);
            auto *dst = output.channel(ch);
            std::memcpy(dst + dst_first, src + src_first, first * sizeof(float));
            if (second)
                std::memcpy(dst + dst_second, src + src_second, second * sizeof(float));
        }
    }

    std::size_t queued(std::size_t write, std::size_t read) const noexcept
    {
        return write >= read ? write - read : 2 * capacity_ + write - read;
    }

    std::size_t advance(std::size_t cursor, std::size_t frames) const noexcept
    {
        const auto next = cursor + frames;
        return next < 2 * capacity_ ? next : next - 2 * capacity_;
    }

    Buffer storage_;
    std::size_t capacity_ = 0;
    std::atomic<std::size_t> read_{0};
    std::atomic<std::size_t> write_{0};
};

} // namespace aereborn

#endif

===== core/graph.h =====
#ifndef AEREBORN_CORE_GRAPH_H
#define AEREBORN_CORE_GRAPH_H

#include "types.h"
#include <array>

namespace aereborn
{

/// @brief Graph execution unit with immutable input and output port layouts.
///
/// Keep the port descriptors and their storage valid and unchanged for as long as
/// the engine owns the node.
/// Defer any requests to control the owning engine and return without waiting for them.
class Node
{
  public:
    virtual ~Node() = default;
    Node(const Node &) = delete;
    Node &operator=(const Node &) = delete;

    /// @brief Return a static diagnostic type name. May be called during processing.
    virtual const char *type_name() const noexcept { return "Node"; }

    /// @brief Describe the input ports.
    /// @return A read-only view of the input port specifications in port index order.
    virtual View<const PortSpec> inputs() const noexcept = 0;

    /// @brief Describe the output ports.
    /// @return A read-only view of the output port specifications in port index order.
    virtual View<const PortSpec> outputs() const noexcept = 0;

    /// @brief Prepare the node's processing resources on the control thread.
    /// @param period Block period validated by the engine.
    /// @throws Error If preparation fails.
    /// @note The default implementation succeeds without preparing any resources.
    /// @note The engine may retry after a failure.
    virtual void prepare(BlockPeriod /*period*/) {}

    /// @brief Process one block and write every output sample in its declared format.
    /// @param context Current period, tick position, port blocks, and per-port activity flags.
    /// @pre Preparation has succeeded.
    /// @note Sources default to active. Other nodes' outputs default to the logical OR of their inputs.
    /// Mark an output active if it produces audio with all inputs inactive. Inactive outputs must be filled with zero bytes.
    /// @note The context and its views are valid only during this call. Keep processing
    /// free of heap allocation and deallocation, blocking I/O, and logging.
    /// Avoid lock waits where possible.
    virtual void process(const ProcessContext &context) noexcept = 0;

    /// @brief Reset the node's processing state.
    /// @pre Reset must be serialized with processing.
    /// @note The engine may reset a node before its first preparation.
    virtual void reset() noexcept
    {
    }

  protected:
    Node() = default;
};

/// @brief Node base with compile-time port counts and inline port specifications.
/// @tparam I Number of input ports.
/// @tparam O Number of output ports.
template <std::size_t I, std::size_t O> class NodeT : public Node
{
    static_assert(I + O > 0, "Node needs at least one port");

  public:
    NodeT(std::array<PortSpec, I> inputs, std::array<PortSpec, O> outputs) : inputs_(inputs), outputs_(outputs)
    {
    }
    View<const PortSpec> inputs() const noexcept final
    {
        return {inputs_.data(), I};
    }
    View<const PortSpec> outputs() const noexcept final
    {
        return {outputs_.data(), O};
    }

  private:
    const std::array<PortSpec, I> inputs_;
    const std::array<PortSpec, O> outputs_;
};

} // namespace aereborn

#endif // AEREBORN_CORE_GRAPH_H

===== core/resampler.cpp =====
#include "resampler.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

namespace aereborn
{
namespace
{

template <std::size_t Bytes> std::uint32_t read_le(const std::uint8_t *input) noexcept
{
    std::uint32_t raw = 0;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < Bytes; ++i)
    {
        raw |= static_cast<std::uint32_t>(input[i]) << (8 * i);
    }
    return raw;
}

template <std::size_t Bytes> void write_le(std::uint32_t raw, std::uint8_t *output) noexcept
{
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < Bytes; ++i)
    {
        output[i] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(raw >> (8 * i));
    }
}

template <unsigned int Bits> float decode_integer(std::uint32_t raw) noexcept
{
    constexpr auto scale = std::int64_t{1} << (Bits - 1);
    const auto value = std::int64_t{raw} & (2 * scale - 1);
    return static_cast<float>(value >= scale ? value - 2 * scale : value) / static_cast<float>(scale);
}

template <unsigned int Bits> std::uint32_t encode_integer(float sample) noexcept
{
    constexpr auto scale = std::int64_t{1} << (Bits - 1);
    const auto normalized = std::isnan(sample) ? 0.0f : std::max(-1.0f, std::min(1.0f, sample));
    const auto rounded = static_cast<std::int64_t>(std::round(static_cast<double>(normalized) * scale));
    const auto value = std::min(scale - 1, rounded);
    return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(value) & static_cast<std::uint32_t>(2 * scale - 1);
}

bool native_f32le() noexcept
{
    const float one = 1.0f;
    constexpr std::uint8_t one_le[] = {0, 0, 0x80, 0x3f};
    return std::memcmp(&one, one_le, sizeof(one)) == 0;
}

void decode_f32le(const std::uint8_t *pcm, OFBlock output) noexcept
{
    if (output.frames() == 0)
        return;
    if (output.channels() == 1 && native_f32le())
    {
        std::memcpy(output.channel(0), pcm, output.frames() * sizeof(float));
        return;
    }
    for (std::size_t frame = 0; frame < output.frames(); ++frame)
    {
        for (std::size_t ch = 0; ch < output.channels(); ++ch, pcm += sizeof(float))
        {
            const auto raw = read_le<sizeof(float)>(pcm);
            std::memcpy(output.channel(ch) + frame, &raw, sizeof(raw));
        }
    }
}

void encode_f32le(IFBlock input, std::uint8_t *pcm) noexcept
{
    if (input.frames() == 0)
        return;
    if (input.channels() == 1 && native_f32le())
    {
        std::memcpy(pcm, input.channel(0), input.frames() * sizeof(float));
        return;
    }
    for (std::size_t frame = 0; frame < input.frames(); ++frame)
    {
        for (std::size_t ch = 0; ch < input.channels(); ++ch, pcm += sizeof(float))
        {
            std::uint32_t raw;
            std::memcpy(&raw, input.channel(ch) + frame, sizeof(raw));
            write_le<sizeof(raw)>(raw, pcm);
        }
    }
}

template <SampleFormat Format> void decode_pcm(const std::uint8_t *source, OFBlock output) noexcept
{
    constexpr auto width = sample_bytes(Format);
    constexpr auto bits = sample_bits(Format);
    for (std::size_t frame = 0; frame < output.frames(); ++frame)
        for (std::size_t ch = 0; ch < output.channels(); ++ch, source += width)
            output.channel(ch)[frame] = decode_integer<bits>(read_le<width>(source));
}

template <SampleFormat Format> void encode_pcm(IFBlock input, std::uint8_t *destination) noexcept
{
    constexpr auto width = sample_bytes(Format);
    constexpr auto bits = sample_bits(Format);
    for (std::size_t frame = 0; frame < input.frames(); ++frame)
        for (std::size_t ch = 0; ch < input.channels(); ++ch, destination += width)
            write_le<width>(encode_integer<bits>(input.channel(ch)[frame]), destination);
}

constexpr std::size_t order = 48;
constexpr std::size_t taps = 2 * order;
constexpr std::uint32_t phase_steps = 100;
constexpr unsigned fraction_bits = 32;
constexpr auto phase_unit = std::uint64_t{1} << fraction_bits;
constexpr double pi = 3.141592653589793;

std::uint64_t step_units(double step) noexcept
{
    return static_cast<std::uint64_t>(step * phase_unit + 0.5);
}

// Window and cutoff ported from AudioDriver's SincInterpolator.
double sinc_kernel(double x, double cutoff) noexcept
{
    if (std::abs(x) >= order)
    {
        return 0.0;
    }
    const auto angle = (2.0 * pi * (x + 1.0 - order)) / (taps - 1);
    const auto window =
        0.3635819 - 0.4891775 * std::cos(angle) + 0.1365995 * std::cos(2 * angle) - 0.0106411 * std::cos(3 * angle);
    const auto sinc_angle = pi * cutoff * x;
    const auto sinc = sinc_angle == 0 ? 1.0 : std::sin(sinc_angle) / sinc_angle;
    return sinc * window;
}

using Coefficients = std::array<float, taps>;

void calc_coffs(const float *kernel, std::size_t phase_index, float phase_frac, Coefficients &coffs) noexcept
{
    const auto *lo_coffs = kernel + phase_index * taps;
    const auto *hi_coffs = lo_coffs + taps;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < taps; ++i)
    {
        coffs[i] = lo_coffs[i] + (hi_coffs[i] - lo_coffs[i]) * phase_frac;
    }
}

float interpolate(const float *history, const float *input, std::size_t history_taps,
                  const Coefficients &coffs) noexcept
{
    float sum0 = 0.0f, sum1 = 0.0f, sum2 = 0.0f, sum3 = 0.0f;
    std::size_t i = 0;
    for (; i + 4 <= history_taps; i += 4)
    {
        sum0 += history[i] * coffs[i];
        sum1 += history[i + 1] * coffs[i + 1];
        sum2 += history[i + 2] * coffs[i + 2];
        sum3 += history[i + 3] * coffs[i + 3];
    }
    for (; i < history_taps; ++i)
        sum0 += history[i] * coffs[i];

    const auto input_taps = taps - history_taps;
    const auto *input_coffs = coffs.data() + history_taps;
    i = 0;
    for (; i + 4 <= input_taps; i += 4)
    {
        sum0 += input[i] * input_coffs[i];
        sum1 += input[i + 1] * input_coffs[i + 1];
        sum2 += input[i + 2] * input_coffs[i + 2];
        sum3 += input[i + 3] * input_coffs[i + 3];
    }
    for (; i < input_taps; ++i)
        sum0 += input[i] * input_coffs[i];
    return (sum0 + sum1) + (sum2 + sum3);
}

void prepare_filter(Buffer &history, std::vector<float> &kernel, std::size_t channels, double cutoff)
{
    history.resize(channels, taps);
    kernel.resize((phase_steps + 1) * taps);
    for (std::uint32_t phase = 0; phase <= phase_steps; ++phase)
    {
        auto *coffs = kernel.data() + phase * taps;
        const auto fraction = static_cast<double>(phase) / phase_steps;
        double scale = 0;
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < taps; ++i)
        {
            coffs[i] = static_cast<float>(sinc_kernel(fraction + order - 1 - i, cutoff));
            scale += coffs[i];
        }
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < taps; ++i)
            coffs[i] = static_cast<float>(coffs[i] / scale);
    }
}

void filter_frame(OFBlock past, const std::vector<float> &kernel, IFBlock input, OFBlock output, std::size_t out_frame,
                  std::size_t through, std::size_t phase_index, float phase_fraction) noexcept
{
    const auto input_taps = std::min(through, taps);
    const auto input_offset = through - input_taps;
    Coefficients coffs;
    calc_coffs(kernel.data(), phase_index, phase_fraction, coffs);
    for (std::size_t ch = 0; ch < output.channels(); ++ch)
    {
        const auto *history = past.channel(ch) + input_taps;
        const auto *samples = through ? input.channel(ch) + input_offset : nullptr;
        output.channel(ch)[out_frame] = interpolate(history, samples, taps - input_taps, coffs);
    }
}

void remember(Buffer &history, IFBlock input, std::size_t consumed) noexcept
{
    if (!consumed)
        return;
    const auto past = history.block(taps);
    const auto copy_frames = std::min(consumed, taps);
    const auto keep_frames = taps - copy_frames;
    for (std::size_t ch = 0; ch < past.channels(); ++ch)
    {
        auto *history_samples = past.channel(ch);
        std::memmove(history_samples, history_samples + copy_frames, keep_frames * sizeof(float));
        std::copy_n(input.channel(ch) + consumed - copy_frames, copy_frames, history_samples + keep_frames);
    }
}

using DecodeFn = void (*)(const std::uint8_t *, OFBlock);
using EncodeFn = void (*)(IFBlock, std::uint8_t *);

std::pair<DecodeFn, EncodeFn> codec_for(SampleFormat format) noexcept
{
    switch (format)
    {
    case SampleFormat::S16LE:
        return {decode_pcm<SampleFormat::S16LE>, encode_pcm<SampleFormat::S16LE>};
    case SampleFormat::S24_3LE:
        return {decode_pcm<SampleFormat::S24_3LE>, encode_pcm<SampleFormat::S24_3LE>};
    case SampleFormat::S24LE:
        return {decode_pcm<SampleFormat::S24LE>, encode_pcm<SampleFormat::S24LE>};
    case SampleFormat::S32LE:
        return {decode_pcm<SampleFormat::S32LE>, encode_pcm<SampleFormat::S32LE>};
    case SampleFormat::F32LE:
        return {decode_f32le, encode_f32le};
    case SampleFormat::F32P:
        break;
    }
    return {nullptr, nullptr};
}

constexpr auto frame_limit = std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / 16;

bool valid_offset(double offset) noexcept
{
    return offset >= 0.0 && offset < 0.5;
}

bool needs_filter(BandWidth src_fs, BandWidth dst_fs, double offset) noexcept
{
    return src_fs != dst_fs || offset > 0;
}

double nominal_step(BandWidth src_fs, BandWidth dst_fs) noexcept
{
    return static_cast<double>(static_cast<std::uint32_t>(src_fs)) / static_cast<std::uint32_t>(dst_fs);
}

std::size_t workspace_bound(BandWidth src_fs, BandWidth dst_fs, std::size_t frames, double offset, bool bound_input)
{
    if (!is_bandwidth(src_fs) || !is_bandwidth(dst_fs) || !valid_offset(offset))
        throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM);
    if (frames > frame_limit)
        throw Error(RetCode::TOOLARGE);
    if (!frames || !needs_filter(src_fs, dst_fs, offset))
        return frames;

    const auto nominal = nominal_step(src_fs, dst_fs);
    const auto step = nominal * (bound_input ? 1 + offset : 1 - offset);
    const auto fixed = static_cast<long double>(step_units(step)) / phase_unit;
    // Cover both the nominal rate envelope and the actual quantized stream step.
    const auto edge = bound_input ? std::max<long double>(step, fixed) : std::min<long double>(step, fixed);
    const auto ratio = bound_input ? edge : 1 / edge;
    // After a capped upsampling call the next output may use the last source frame in history.
    const auto history = bound_input ? 0.0L : std::max(0.0L, 1 - edge);
    const auto count = static_cast<long double>(frames) + history;
    auto scaled = count * ratio;
    // For exceptionally large bounds, round outward even when long double has double precision.
    const auto precision_limit = std::ldexp(1.0L, std::numeric_limits<long double>::digits - 3);
    if (count >= precision_limit || scaled >= precision_limit)
    {
        const auto infinity = std::numeric_limits<long double>::infinity();
        scaled = std::nextafter(std::nextafter(count, infinity) * std::nextafter(ratio, infinity), infinity);
    }
    const auto rounded = std::ceil(scaled);
    if (rounded >= static_cast<long double>(frame_limit))
        throw Error(RetCode::TOOLARGE);
    return static_cast<std::size_t>(rounded) + 1;
}
} // namespace

void decode_pcm(IBBlock input, OFBlock output) noexcept
{
    const auto codec = codec_for(input.spec().format).first;
    assert(codec && input.spec().channels == output.channels() && input.frames() == output.frames());
    codec(input.byte_view().data(), output);
}

void encode_pcm(IFBlock input, OBBlock output) noexcept
{
    const auto codec = codec_for(output.spec().format).second;
    assert(codec && output.spec().channels == input.channels() && output.frames() == input.frames());
    codec(input, output.byte_view().data());
}

void SincResampler::Position::advance(std::uint64_t step, std::uint64_t unit) noexcept
{
    const auto next = fraction + step;
    through += static_cast<std::size_t>(next / unit);
    fraction = next % unit;
}

void SincResampler::prepare(std::size_t channels, BandWidth src_fs, BandWidth dst_fs, double offset)
{
    if (!channels || !is_bandwidth(src_fs) || !is_bandwidth(dst_fs) || !valid_offset(offset))
        throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM);

    Buffer history;
    std::vector<float> kernel;
    const auto fir = needs_filter(src_fs, dst_fs, offset);
    const auto nominal = nominal_step(src_fs, dst_fs);
    if (fir)
    {
        const auto cutoff =
            static_cast<double>(static_cast<std::uint32_t>(dst_fs)) / static_cast<std::uint32_t>(src_fs) / (1 + offset);
        prepare_filter(history, kernel, channels, std::min(1.0, cutoff) * 0.98);
    }
    history_ = std::move(history);
    kernel_ = std::move(kernel);
    need_fir_ = fir;
    channels_ = channels;
    src_fs_ = src_fs;
    dst_fs_ = dst_fs;
    nominal_ = nominal;
    max_rate_offset_ = offset;
    position_ = {};
}

std::size_t SincResampler::tail_frames() const noexcept
{
    return need_fir_ ? taps : 0;
}

void SincResampler::reset() noexcept
{
    position_ = {};
    history_.clear();
}

void SincResampler::process_tick(IFBlock input, OFBlock output, BlockPeriod period, std::uint64_t tick) noexcept
{
    assert(channels_ && max_rate_offset_ == 0 && input.channels() == channels_ && output.channels() == channels_);
    const auto src_fs = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(src_fs_);
    const auto dst_fs = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(dst_fs_);
    const auto rounding = [period, tick](std::uint32_t rate) {
        const auto rem = (tick % 1000) * ((std::uint64_t{rate} * static_cast<std::uint32_t>(period)) % 1000) % 1000;
        return static_cast<std::int64_t>((1000 - rem) % 1000);
    };
    const auto phase = (rounding(dst_fs) * src_fs - rounding(src_fs) * dst_fs) / 1000;
    const auto start = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(dst_fs + phase);
    Position position{static_cast<std::size_t>(start / dst_fs), start % dst_fs};
    const auto result = run(input, output, position, src_fs, dst_fs, true);
    assert(result.produced == output.frames());
    (void)result;
}

std::size_t SincResampler::required_input(std::size_t frames, double scale) const noexcept
{
    assert(channels_ && scale >= 1 - max_rate_offset_ && scale <= 1 + max_rate_offset_);
    assert(frames <= frame_limit);
    if (!frames || !need_fir_)
        return frames;

    const auto step = step_units(nominal_ * scale);
    const auto whole = step >> fraction_bits;
    const auto count = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(frames - 1);
    assert(!whole || count <= frame_limit / whole);
    const auto frac = std::uint64_t{static_cast<std::uint32_t>(step)};
    const auto low = position_.fraction + frac * static_cast<std::uint32_t>(count);
    const auto needed = position_.through + count * whole + (count >> fraction_bits) * frac + (low >> fraction_bits);
    assert(needed <= frame_limit);
    return static_cast<std::size_t>(needed);
}

SincResampler::Transfer SincResampler::process(IFBlock input, OFBlock output, double scale) noexcept
{
    assert(channels_ && scale >= 1 - max_rate_offset_ && scale <= 1 + max_rate_offset_);
    assert(input.channels() == channels_ && output.channels() == channels_);
    assert(input.frames() <= frame_limit && output.frames() <= frame_limit);
    assert(input.valid() && output.valid());
    return run(input, output, position_, step_units(nominal_ * scale), phase_unit, false);
}

SincResampler::Transfer SincResampler::run(IFBlock input, OFBlock output, Position &phase, std::uint64_t step,
                                           std::uint64_t unit, bool tick) noexcept
{
    if (!need_fir_)
    {
        const auto frames = std::min(input.frames(), output.frames());
        if (frames)
            for (std::size_t ch = 0; ch < channels_; ++ch)
                std::memcpy(output.channel(ch), input.channel(ch), frames * sizeof(float));
        return {frames, frames};
    }
    if (!output.frames() && !tick)
        return {};

    const auto past = history_.block(taps);
    Transfer result;
    std::size_t last = 0;
    while (result.produced < output.frames() && phase.through <= input.frames())
    {
        last = phase.through;
        const auto phase_pos = phase.fraction * phase_steps;
        const auto index = static_cast<std::size_t>(phase_pos / unit);
        const auto blend = static_cast<float>(phase_pos % unit) / static_cast<float>(unit);
        filter_frame(past, kernel_, input, output, result.produced, last, index, blend);
        ++result.produced;
        phase.advance(step, unit);
    }

    // The graph supplies a new block every tick. A stream keeps unconsumed input with the caller.
    result.consumed = input.frames();
    if (!tick)
    {
        const auto skipped = phase.through ? phase.through - 1 : 0;
        result.consumed = std::min(input.frames(), std::max(last, skipped));
        phase.through -= result.consumed;
    }
    remember(history_, input, result.consumed);
    return result;
}

std::size_t SincResampler::max_output_frames(BandWidth src_fs, BandWidth dst_fs, std::size_t frames, double offset)
{
    return workspace_bound(src_fs, dst_fs, frames, offset, false);
}

std::size_t SincResampler::max_input_frames(BandWidth src_fs, BandWidth dst_fs, std::size_t frames, double offset)
{
    return workspace_bound(src_fs, dst_fs, frames, offset, true);
}

} // namespace aereborn

===== core/resampler.h =====
#ifndef AEREBORN_CORE_RESAMPLER_H
#define AEREBORN_CORE_RESAMPLER_H

#include "types.h"
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>

namespace aereborn
{

constexpr std::uint64_t max_clock_drift_ppm = 2000;

/// @brief Decode interleaved PCM frames into planar F32P samples.
/// @pre is_interleaved_pcm(input.spec().format), matching channels and frames, non-overlapping buffers.
void decode_pcm(IBBlock input, OFBlock output) noexcept;

/// @brief Encode planar F32P samples into interleaved PCM frames.
/// @pre is_interleaved_pcm(output.spec().format), matching channels and frames, non-overlapping buffers.
void encode_pcm(IFBlock input, OBBlock output) noexcept;

/// @brief Polyphase windowed-sinc resampler operating on planar F32P blocks.
class SincResampler
{
  public:
    struct Transfer
    {
        std::size_t consumed = 0;
        std::size_t produced = 0;
    };

    /// @brief Prepare filter kernel and history for channels, rates, and rate offset in [0, 0.5).
    /// @note Replaces filter storage and resets state on success; failure preserves previous state.
    void prepare(std::size_t channels, BandWidth src_fs, BandWidth dst_fs, double max_rate_offset = 0.0);

    /// @brief Zero input frames sufficient to flush the FIR history at end of stream.
    std::size_t tail_frames() const noexcept;

    /// @brief Clear FIR history and reset stream phase while keeping prepared storage.
    void reset() noexcept;

    /// @brief True when rate conversion or non-zero rate offset requires FIR interpolation.
    bool needs_fir() const noexcept { return need_fir_; }

    /// @brief Synchronous graph tick with exact rational phase alignment.
    /// @pre Prepared for a fixed ratio. Blocks match the prepared channels and the tick geometry.
    void process_tick(IFBlock input, OFBlock output, BlockPeriod period, std::uint64_t tick) noexcept;

    /// @brief Exact input frames needed at current phase to produce output_frames.
    /// @pre Prepared, rate_scale within the prepared range, and frame counts within the supported bounds.
    std::size_t required_input(std::size_t output_frames, double rate_scale = 1.0) const noexcept;

    /// @brief Variable-rate streaming resample at nominal step * rate_scale.
    /// @pre Prepared, rate_scale within the prepared range, and frame counts within the supported bounds.
    /// @pre Valid input and output blocks match the prepared channel count and do not overlap.
    /// @note With the same scale and phase, all offered input is consumed if its size
    ///       does not exceed required_input(output.frames(), rate_scale).
    Transfer process(IFBlock input, OFBlock output, double rate_scale = 1.0) noexcept;

    /// @brief Bound streaming output for the given input batch and rate offset.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPARAM for invalid rates or offset, or RetCode::TOOLARGE for an unsupported frame count.
    static std::size_t max_output_frames(BandWidth src_fs, BandWidth dst_fs, std::size_t input_frames,
                                         double max_rate_offset = 0.0);
    /// @brief Bound streaming input for the given output batch and rate offset.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPARAM for invalid rates or offset, or RetCode::TOOLARGE for an unsupported frame count.
    static std::size_t max_input_frames(BandWidth src_fs, BandWidth dst_fs, std::size_t output_frames,
                                        double max_rate_offset = 0.0);

  private:
    struct Position
    {
        std::size_t through = 1;
        std::uint64_t fraction = 0;
        void advance(std::uint64_t step, std::uint64_t unit) noexcept;
    };

    Transfer run(IFBlock input, OFBlock output, Position &phase, std::uint64_t step,
                 std::uint64_t unit, bool tick) noexcept;

    bool need_fir_ = false;
    std::size_t channels_ = 0;
    BandWidth src_fs_{};
    BandWidth dst_fs_{};
    double nominal_ = 0.0;
    double max_rate_offset_ = 0.0;
    Position position_;
    Buffer history_;
    std::vector<float> kernel_;
};

} // namespace aereborn

#endif

===== core/retcode.h =====
#ifndef AEREBORN_CORE_RETCODE_H
#define AEREBORN_CORE_RETCODE_H

#include "audio_types.h"
#include <cassert>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <type_traits>
#include <utility>

namespace aereborn
{

/// @brief Exception for AEReborn control-side validation and operation failures.
class Error : public std::runtime_error
{
  public:
    explicit Error(RetCode code) : std::runtime_error(code.what()), code_(code) {}
    Error(RetCode code, const char *detail) : std::runtime_error(detail), code_(code) {}
    Error(RetCode code, const std::string &detail) : std::runtime_error(detail), code_(code) {}

    RetCode::Code code() const noexcept { return code_.code(); }

  private:
    RetCode code_;
};

/// @brief Throw Error if the operation did not return OK.
inline void throw_if_error(RetCode code)
{
    if (!code)
        throw Error(code);
}

/// @brief Status or value for backend operations. Check the status before reading the value.
template <class T> class Result
{
  public:
    Result(T value) noexcept(std::is_nothrow_move_constructible<T>::value) : value_(std::move(value))
    {
    }
    Result(RetCode code) noexcept(std::is_nothrow_default_constructible<T>::value) : ret_code_(code)
    {
        assert(!code);
    }
    Result(RetCode::Code code) noexcept(std::is_nothrow_default_constructible<T>::value) : Result(RetCode(code))
    {
    }
    RetCode ret_code() const noexcept
    {
        return ret_code_;
    }
    explicit operator bool() const noexcept
    {
        return static_cast<bool>(ret_code_);
    }
    T &value() & noexcept
    {
        assert(ret_code_);
        return value_;
    }
    const T &value() const & noexcept
    {
        assert(ret_code_);
        return value_;
    }
    T value() && noexcept(std::is_nothrow_move_constructible<T>::value)
    {
        assert(ret_code_);
        return std::move(value_);
    }

    /// @brief Move out the result or throw Error on failure.
    T value_or_throw() &&
    {
        throw_if_error(ret_code_);
        return std::move(value_);
    }

  private:
    RetCode ret_code_;
    T value_{};
};

/// @brief Run a non-throwing cleanup when leaving scope unless dismissed.
template <class Cleanup> class ScopeExit
{
  public:
    explicit ScopeExit(Cleanup cleanup) noexcept(std::is_nothrow_move_constructible<Cleanup>::value)
        : cleanup_(std::move(cleanup))
    {
    }
    ScopeExit(const ScopeExit &) = delete;
    ScopeExit &operator=(const ScopeExit &) = delete;

    ~ScopeExit()
    {
        if (active_)
            cleanup_();
    }

    void dismiss() noexcept { active_ = false; }

  private:
    Cleanup cleanup_;
    bool active_ = true;
};

} // namespace aereborn

#endif // AEREBORN_CORE_RETCODE_H

===== core/types.cpp =====
#include "types.h"

#include <algorithm>

namespace aereborn
{

void Buffer::resize(std::size_t channels, std::size_t capacity_frames)
{
    if (channels == 0 || capacity_frames == 0)
        throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM);
    if (channels > samples_.max_size() / capacity_frames)
        throw Error(RetCode::TOOLARGE);
    const auto sample_count = channels * capacity_frames;
    const auto retained_samples = std::min(samples_.size(), sample_count);
    samples_.resize(sample_count);
    std::fill_n(samples_.begin(), retained_samples, 0.0f);
    channels_ = channels;
    capacity_frames_ = capacity_frames;
}

OFBlock Buffer::block(std::size_t frames) noexcept
{
    return {samples_.data(), channels_, frames, capacity_frames_};
}

void Buffer::clear() noexcept
{
    std::fill(samples_.begin(), samples_.end(), 0.0f);
}

constexpr std::uint64_t NodeId::port_mask;
constexpr std::uint64_t NodeId::max_node;
constexpr std::size_t NodeId::invalid_port;

bool PortSpec::valid() const noexcept
{
    return is_bandwidth(bandwidth) && channels > 0 && sample_bytes(format) != 0;
}

} // namespace aereborn

===== core/types.h =====
#ifndef AEREBORN_CORE_TYPES_H
#define AEREBORN_CORE_TYPES_H

#include "audio_types.h"
#include "block.h"
#include <cstdint>

namespace aereborn
{

constexpr bool is_bandwidth(BandWidth bandwidth) noexcept
{
    switch (bandwidth)
    {
    case BandWidth::Narrow:
    case BandWidth::QuarterCD:
    case BandWidth::Wide:
    case BandWidth::HalfCD:
    case BandWidth::SemiSuperWide:
    case BandWidth::SuperWideband:
    case BandWidth::CDQuality:
    case BandWidth::Full:
    case BandWidth::DoubleCD:
    case BandWidth::UltraFull:
        return true;
    case BandWidth::Unknown:
        break;
    }
    return false;
}
constexpr bool is_block_period(BlockPeriod period) noexcept
{
    return period == BlockPeriod::INR_02MS || period == BlockPeriod::INR_05MS || period == BlockPeriod::INR_10MS ||
           period == BlockPeriod::INR_20MS || period == BlockPeriod::INR_40MS;
}
constexpr unsigned int sample_bits(SampleFormat format) noexcept
{
    switch (format)
    {
    case SampleFormat::S16LE:
        return 16;
    case SampleFormat::S24_3LE:
    case SampleFormat::S24LE:
        return 24;
    case SampleFormat::F32P:
    case SampleFormat::S32LE:
    case SampleFormat::F32LE:
        return 32;
    }
    return 0;
}
constexpr std::size_t sample_bytes(SampleFormat format) noexcept
{
    return format == SampleFormat::S24LE ? 4 : sample_bits(format) / 8;
}

/// @brief Check whether format is a supported interleaved little-endian PCM format.
constexpr bool is_interleaved_pcm(SampleFormat format) noexcept
{
    return format != SampleFormat::F32P && sample_bytes(format) != 0;
}

inline bool valid_pcm_spec(PortSpec spec) noexcept
{
    return spec.format != SampleFormat::F32P && spec.valid();
}

/// @brief Borrow audio through a byte view carrying the port specification and frame count.
/// @tparam Byte Use std::uint8_t for writable bytes or const std::uint8_t for read-only bytes.
/// @note The port specification determines how to interpret the samples.
///
/// The render plan owns the storage provided to a node. Use its views only for the
/// duration of the process call.
template <class Byte> class ByteBlock
{
    template <class> friend class ByteBlock;
    using Float = typename std::conditional<std::is_const<Byte>::value, const float, float>::type;

  public:
    ByteBlock() noexcept = default;

    /// @brief Interpret a borrowed buffer according to the port specification.
    /// @param data Address of the buffer.
    /// @param spec Sample rate, channel count, and format of the buffer.
    /// @param frames Number of frames available in the buffer for this block.
    /// @pre A nonempty block refers to storage large enough for the specified frames.
    ByteBlock(Byte *data, PortSpec spec, std::size_t frames) noexcept : data_(data), spec_(spec), frames_(frames)
    {
    }
    template <class OtherByte, typename std::enable_if<std::is_convertible<OtherByte *, Byte *>::value, int>::type = 0>
    ByteBlock(ByteBlock<OtherByte> other) noexcept : data_(other.data_), spec_(other.spec_), frames_(other.frames_)
    {
    }
    PortSpec spec() const noexcept
    {
        return spec_;
    }
    std::size_t frames() const noexcept
    {
        return frames_;
    }
    View<Byte> byte_view() const noexcept
    {
        return {data_, frames_ * spec_.channels * sample_bytes(spec_.format)};
    }
    FloatBlock<Float> f32p_view() const noexcept
    {
        assert(spec_.format == SampleFormat::F32P);
        return {reinterpret_cast<Float *>(data_), spec_.channels, frames_, frames_};
    }
  private:
    Byte *data_ = nullptr;
    PortSpec spec_;
    std::size_t frames_ = 0;
};

using IBBlock = ByteBlock<const std::uint8_t>;
using OBBlock = ByteBlock<std::uint8_t>;

/// @brief Identity assigned to one connection when it is created.
/// @note Zero is the empty ID. Reconnecting the same ports creates a new ID.
struct ConnId
{
    std::uint64_t value = 0;
    constexpr explicit operator bool() const noexcept
    {
        return value != 0;
    }
    constexpr bool operator==(ConnId other) const noexcept
    {
        return value == other.value;
    }
    constexpr bool operator!=(ConnId other) const noexcept
    {
        return !(*this == other);
    }
    constexpr bool operator<(ConnId other) const noexcept
    {
        return value < other.value;
    }
};

/// @brief Directed connection in a committed render plan.
struct Connection
{
    ConnId id;
    NodeId from;
    NodeId to;
};

/// @brief Timing, port blocks, and activity flags passed to a node for one process call.
/// @note All views and their backing storage are borrowed for the duration of that call.
/// @note Each supplied activity view has one flag per corresponding port.
struct ProcessContext
{
    BlockPeriod block_period;                ///< Duration of the current engine tick.
    std::uint64_t tick_position;             ///< Zero-based position of the current tick.
    View<const IBBlock> inputs;              ///< Read-only input blocks in port index order.
    View<const OBBlock> outputs;             ///< Output blocks with writable samples in port index order.
    View<const std::uint8_t> input_active{}; ///< Per-input flags. Zero guarantees a zero-filled block.
    View<std::uint8_t> output_active{};      ///< Per-output flags initialized by the engine.

    /// @brief Check whether the input block should be processed as active.
    /// @pre index is a valid input port index.
    /// @note Returns true when a standalone caller omits the activity view.
    bool has_input(std::size_t index) const noexcept
    {
        return input_active.empty() || input_active[index] != 0;
    }

    /// @brief Set the output block's activity flag.
    /// @pre index is a valid output port index.
    /// @note An inactive output must be filled with zero bytes. With no activity view, this is a no-op.
    void set_output_active(std::size_t index, bool active) const noexcept
    {
        if (!output_active.empty())
            output_active[index] = active ? 1 : 0;
    }
};

} // namespace aereborn

#endif

===== plugins/convert.cpp =====
#include "convert.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstring>

namespace aereborn
{

Convert::Convert(PortSpec input, PortSpec output)
    : NodeT({input}, {output}), same_spec_(input == output)
{
    if (!input.valid() || !output.valid() || input.channels != output.channels)
        throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM, "Invalid converter port specifications");
}

void Convert::prepare(BlockPeriod period)
{
    if (!is_block_period(period))
        throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM);
    if (same_spec_)
        return;
    const auto input = inputs()[0], output = outputs()[0];
    const auto in_pcm = input.format != SampleFormat::F32P;
    const auto out_pcm = output.format != SampleFormat::F32P;
    const auto fir = input.bandwidth != output.bandwidth;
    Buffer decoded, resampled;
    if (in_pcm && (fir || out_pcm))
        decoded.resize(input.channels, input.max_frames(period));
    if (fir)
    {
        if (out_pcm)
            resampled.resize(output.channels, output.max_frames(period));
        resampler_.prepare(input.channels, input.bandwidth, output.bandwidth);
    }
    decoded_ = std::move(decoded);
    resampled_ = std::move(resampled);
    tail_remaining_ = 0;
}

void Convert::process(const ProcessContext &context) noexcept
{
    const auto input = context.inputs[0];
    const auto output = context.outputs[0];
    const bool active = context.has_input(0);
    if (!active && tail_remaining_ == 0)
    {
        context.set_output_active(0, false);
        const auto bytes = output.byte_view();
        if (!bytes.empty())
            std::memset(bytes.data(), 0, bytes.size());
        return;
    }
    context.set_output_active(0, true);

    if (same_spec_)
    {
        // The byte copy preserves float bit patterns and PCM padding.
        const auto bytes = input.byte_view();
        if (!bytes.empty())
            std::memcpy(output.byte_view().data(), bytes.data(), bytes.size());
        return;
    }

    const auto in_pcm = input.spec().format != SampleFormat::F32P;
    const auto out_pcm = output.spec().format != SampleFormat::F32P;
    const auto fir = resampler_.needs_fir();
    IFBlock source;
    if (in_pcm)
    {
        const auto decoded = fir || out_pcm ? decoded_.block(input.frames()) : output.f32p_view();
        decode_pcm(input, decoded);
        source = decoded;
    }
    else
        source = input.f32p_view();

    if (fir)
    {
        // The block that finishes the flush may still contain tail samples.
        tail_remaining_ =
            active ? resampler_.tail_frames() : tail_remaining_ - std::min(tail_remaining_, input.frames());
        const auto dest = out_pcm ? resampled_.block(output.frames()) : output.f32p_view();
        resampler_.process_tick(source, dest, context.block_period, context.tick_position);
        source = dest;
    }
    if (out_pcm)
        encode_pcm(source, output);
}

void Convert::reset() noexcept
{
    resampler_.reset();
    tail_remaining_ = 0;
}

} // namespace aereborn

===== plugins/convert.h =====
#ifndef AEREBORN_PLUGINS_CONVERT_H
#define AEREBORN_PLUGINS_CONVERT_H

#include "core/graph.h"
#include "core/resampler.h"

namespace aereborn
{

/// @brief Convert sample format and sample rate synchronously inside an Engine graph.
/// @note Port specifications stay fixed. Use Matrix to change the channel count.
class Convert final : public NodeT<1, 1>
{
  public:
    /// @copydoc Node::type_name
    const char *type_name() const noexcept override { return "Convert"; }

    /// @brief Construct a converter with fixed input and output specifications.
    /// @param input Source sample format, sample rate, and channel count.
    /// @param output Destination specification with the same channel count.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPARAM If either spec is invalid or the channel counts differ.
    Convert(PortSpec input, PortSpec output);

    /// @brief Prepare conversion buffers and reset the resampler for the graph period.
    /// @param period Graph block period used to size the buffers.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPARAM for an unsupported period, or RetCode::TOOLARGE if a buffer exceeds its limit.
    /// @note A failed preparation preserves the existing buffers and resampler state.
    void prepare(BlockPeriod period) override;

    /// @copydoc Node::process
    /// @pre The blocks match the declared port specifications.
    /// @note Resampling aligns its fractional phase to the graph tick.
    void process(const ProcessContext &context) noexcept override;

    /// @brief Clear resampling history and phase while keeping prepared storage.
    /// @pre Serialize with process().
    void reset() noexcept override;

  private:
    bool same_spec_ = false;
    std::size_t tail_remaining_ = 0; ///< Zero input frames still needed to clear FIR history.
    SincResampler resampler_;
    Buffer decoded_;   ///< Allocated only when input is PCM and (resampler_.needs_fir() || output is different PCM).
    Buffer resampled_; ///< Allocated only when resampler_.needs_fir() && output is PCM.
};

} // namespace aereborn

#endif

===== plugins/endpoint.cpp =====
#include "endpoint.h"

#include "core/resampler.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <limits>

namespace aereborn
{
namespace
{
constexpr unsigned graph_open = 1u;
constexpr unsigned graph_active = 2u;

PortSpec endpoint_spec(BandWidth rate, std::size_t channels, std::chrono::microseconds io_period)
{
    const PortSpec spec(rate, channels, SampleFormat::F32P);
    if (!spec.valid() || io_period.count() <= 0)
        throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM, "Invalid endpoint port or I/O period");
    return spec;
}

void prepare_ring(FrameRing &ring, PortSpec spec, BlockPeriod period, std::chrono::microseconds io_period,
                  BlockPeriod &prepared_period, bool in_use)
{
    if (!is_block_period(period))
        throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM);
    if (period == prepared_period)
        return;
    if (in_use)
        throw Error(RetCode::BUSY);
    const auto graph_frames = spec.max_frames(period);
    const auto rate = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(spec.bandwidth);
    const auto us = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(io_period.count());
    if (us > std::numeric_limits<std::uint64_t>::max() / rate)
        throw Error(RetCode::TOOLARGE);
    // Round up at the slowest allowed device clock to cover the largest logical batch.
    constexpr auto effective_second = 1000000 - max_clock_drift_ppm;
    const auto units = us * rate;
    const auto device_frames = units / effective_second + (units % effective_second != 0);
    const auto capacity = std::max<std::uint64_t>(8 * graph_frames, 2 * device_frames + 4 * graph_frames);
    if (capacity > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max())
        throw Error(RetCode::TOOLARGE);
    ring.prepare(spec.channels, static_cast<std::size_t>(capacity));
    prepared_period = period;
}

bool enter_graph(std::atomic<unsigned> &state) noexcept
{
    auto expected = graph_open;
    return state.compare_exchange_strong(expected, graph_open | graph_active, std::memory_order_acquire,
                                         std::memory_order_relaxed);
}

void close_graph_gate(std::atomic<unsigned> &state) noexcept
{
    state.fetch_and(~graph_open, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
    // Only the caller outside the graph waits, until the current queue access ends.
    while (state.load(std::memory_order_acquire) & graph_active)
    {
    }
}

void open_graph_gate(std::atomic<unsigned> &state, std::atomic<bool> &primed, bool prefill) noexcept
{
    primed.store(!prefill, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    state.store(graph_open, std::memory_order_release);
}

bool prefill_ready(const FrameRing &ring, std::atomic<bool> &primed, std::size_t target_frames) noexcept
{
    if (primed.load(std::memory_order_relaxed))
        return true;
    if (ring.available() < target_frames)
        return false;
    primed.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
    return true;
}
} // namespace

Capture::Capture(BandWidth rate, std::size_t channels, std::chrono::microseconds io_period)
    : NodeT({}, {endpoint_spec(rate, channels, io_period)}), io_period_(io_period)
{
}

void Capture::prepare(BlockPeriod period)
{
    prepare_ring(ring_, spec(), period, io_period_, period_, bound());
}

void Capture::process(const ProcessContext &context) noexcept
{
    const auto output = context.outputs[0].f32p_view();
    std::size_t frames = 0;
    if (enter_graph(graph_state_))
    {
        if (prefill_ready(ring_, primed_, target_frames()))
        {
            frames = ring_.read(output);
            if (frames < output.frames())
                underrun_frames_.fetch_add(output.frames() - frames, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        }
        graph_state_.fetch_and(~graph_active, std::memory_order_release);
    }
    output.subblock(frames, output.frames() - frames).clear();
    context.set_output_active(0, frames != 0);
}

bool Capture::online() const noexcept
{
    return (graph_state_.load(std::memory_order_acquire) & graph_open) != 0;
}

void Capture::open(bool prefill) noexcept
{
    underrun_frames_.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    open_graph_gate(graph_state_, primed_, prefill);
}

void Capture::close() noexcept
{
    close_graph_gate(graph_state_);
    ring_.clear();
    primed_.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
}

Playback::Playback(BandWidth rate, std::size_t channels, std::chrono::microseconds io_period)
    : NodeT({endpoint_spec(rate, channels, io_period)}, {}), io_period_(io_period)
{
}

void Playback::prepare(BlockPeriod period)
{
    prepare_ring(ring_, spec(), period, io_period_, period_, bound());
}

void Playback::process(const ProcessContext &context) noexcept
{
    if (enter_graph(graph_state_))
    {
        const auto input = context.inputs[0].f32p_view();
        const auto written = ring_.write(input);
        if (written < input.frames())
            overflow_frames_.fetch_add(input.frames() - written, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        graph_state_.fetch_and(~graph_active, std::memory_order_release);
    }
}

bool Playback::online() const noexcept
{
    return (graph_state_.load(std::memory_order_acquire) & graph_open) != 0;
}

void Playback::open(bool prefill) noexcept
{
    overflow_frames_.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    open_graph_gate(graph_state_, primed_, prefill);
}

void Playback::close_graph() noexcept
{
    close_graph_gate(graph_state_);
}

void Playback::close() noexcept
{
    close_graph();
    ring_.clear();
    primed_.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
}

std::size_t Playback::read(OFBlock output) noexcept
{
    return prefill_ready(ring_, primed_, target_frames()) ? ring_.read(output) : 0;
}

} // namespace aereborn

===== plugins/endpoint.h =====
#ifndef AEREBORN_PLUGINS_ENDPOINT_H
#define AEREBORN_PLUGINS_ENDPOINT_H

#include "core/fring.h"
#include "core/graph.h"
#include <atomic>
#include <chrono>
#include <cstddef>

namespace aereborn
{

class Backend;

/// @brief Graph-side capture node receiving planar F32P frames from its bound backend.
/// @note The graph is the sole consumer. Serialize prepare() with binding.
class Capture final : public NodeT<0, 1>
{
  public:
    /// @brief Construct a capture node with one fixed F32P output.
    /// @param rate Graph-side sample rate.
    /// @param channels Number of output channels.
    /// @param io_period Maximum duration of one external batch, used to size the queue.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPARAM If the port specification is invalid or the I/O period is nonpositive.
    Capture(BandWidth rate, std::size_t channels, std::chrono::microseconds io_period);

    /// @brief Prepare the queue for the graph period on the control side.
    /// @param period Graph block period.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPARAM for an unsupported period, RetCode::BUSY if changing it while bound, or RetCode::TOOLARGE if the
    /// queue exceeds its capacity limit.
    void prepare(BlockPeriod period) override;

    /// @copydoc Node::process
    /// @note Fills missing output frames with silence, including while offline or waiting for prefill.
    void process(const ProcessContext &context) noexcept override;

    /// @brief Return the fixed F32P output specification.
    PortSpec spec() const noexcept { return outputs()[0]; }

    /// @copydoc Node::type_name
    const char *type_name() const noexcept override { return "Capture"; }

    /// @brief Report whether a backend has claimed this endpoint.
    bool bound() const noexcept { return backend_bound_.load(std::memory_order_acquire); }

    /// @brief Report whether the graph gate is open.
    bool online() const noexcept;

    /// @brief Report whether the queue has released or bypassed its startup prefill gate.
    bool steady() const noexcept { return primed_.load(std::memory_order_acquire); }

    /// @brief Return the external batch duration budget used to size the queue.
    std::chrono::microseconds io_period() const noexcept { return io_period_; }

    /// @brief Return the queue capacity in frames, or zero before preparation.
    /// @pre Serialize with prepare().
    std::size_t capacity() const noexcept { return ring_.capacity(); }

    /// @brief Sample queue occupancy without blocking the graph or I/O.
    /// @return False if a usable estimate is unavailable.
    /// @pre Serialize with prepare().
    bool estimate_queued_frames(std::size_t &frames) const noexcept { return ring_.estimate(frames); }

    /// @brief Count missing output frames on the graph side since open or reset.
    std::size_t underrun_frames() const noexcept { return underrun_frames_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed); }

    /// @copydoc Node::reset
    void reset() noexcept override { underrun_frames_.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed); }

  private:
    friend class Backend;

    /// @brief Allow graph reads. With prefill enabled, reads wait for half the queue capacity.
    /// @pre The backend is bound and the graph gate is closed.
    /// @note Called by the I/O worker before its first write.
    void open(bool prefill) noexcept;

    /// @brief Close graph reads, wait for queue access to finish, and discard remaining frames.
    /// @note Called by the I/O worker after its last write.
    void close() noexcept;

    /// @brief Queue as many input frames as space allows, from the I/O worker.
    void write(IFBlock input) noexcept { ring_.write(input); }

    /// @brief Return the half-capacity prefill and drift target in frames.
    /// @pre Serialize with prepare().
    std::size_t target_frames() const noexcept { return ring_.capacity() / 2; }

    /// @brief Return the current number of queued frames.
    /// @pre Call from the external producer or while both sides are idle.
    std::size_t available() const noexcept { return ring_.available(); }

    const std::chrono::microseconds io_period_;
    BlockPeriod period_{};
    FrameRing ring_;
    std::atomic<bool> backend_bound_{false};
    std::atomic<unsigned> graph_state_{0};
    std::atomic<bool> primed_{false};
    std::atomic<std::size_t> underrun_frames_{0};
};

/// @brief Graph-side playback node delivering planar F32P frames to its bound backend.
/// @note The graph is the sole producer. Serialize prepare() with binding.
class Playback final : public NodeT<1, 0>
{
  public:
    /// @brief Construct a playback node with one fixed F32P input.
    /// @param rate Graph-side sample rate.
    /// @param channels Number of input channels.
    /// @param io_period Maximum duration of one external batch, used to size the queue.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPARAM If the port specification is invalid or the I/O period is nonpositive.
    Playback(BandWidth rate, std::size_t channels, std::chrono::microseconds io_period);

    /// @brief Prepare the queue for the graph period on the control side.
    /// @param period Graph block period.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPARAM for an unsupported period, RetCode::BUSY if changing it while bound, or RetCode::TOOLARGE if the
    /// queue exceeds its capacity limit.
    void prepare(BlockPeriod period) override;

    /// @copydoc Node::process
    /// @note Copies graph input while graph writes are enabled and space is available.
    void process(const ProcessContext &context) noexcept override;

    /// @brief Return the fixed F32P input specification.
    PortSpec spec() const noexcept { return inputs()[0]; }

    /// @copydoc Node::type_name
    const char *type_name() const noexcept override { return "Playback"; }

    /// @brief Report whether a backend has claimed this endpoint.
    bool bound() const noexcept { return backend_bound_.load(std::memory_order_acquire); }

    /// @brief Report whether the graph gate is open.
    bool online() const noexcept;

    /// @brief Report whether the queue has released or bypassed its startup prefill gate.
    bool steady() const noexcept { return primed_.load(std::memory_order_acquire); }

    /// @brief Return the external batch duration budget used to size the queue.
    std::chrono::microseconds io_period() const noexcept { return io_period_; }

    /// @brief Return the queue capacity in frames, or zero before preparation.
    /// @pre Serialize with prepare().
    std::size_t capacity() const noexcept { return ring_.capacity(); }

    /// @brief Sample queue occupancy without blocking the graph or I/O.
    /// @return False if a usable estimate is unavailable.
    /// @pre Serialize with prepare().
    bool estimate_queued_frames(std::size_t &frames) const noexcept { return ring_.estimate(frames); }

    /// @brief Count discarded input frames on the graph side since open or reset.
    std::size_t overflow_frames() const noexcept { return overflow_frames_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed); }

    /// @copydoc Node::reset
    void reset() noexcept override { overflow_frames_.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed); }

  private:
    friend class Backend;

    /// @brief Allow graph writes. With prefill enabled, I/O reads wait for half the queue capacity.
    /// @pre The backend is bound and the graph gate is closed.
    /// @note Called by the I/O worker before its first read.
    void open(bool prefill) noexcept;

    /// @brief Close graph writes and wait for queue access to finish, leaving frames for the I/O worker.
    void close_graph() noexcept;

    /// @brief Close graph writes, wait for queue access to finish, and discard remaining frames.
    /// @note Called by the I/O worker after its last read.
    void close() noexcept;

    /// @brief Copy available planar F32P frames from the queue into the output view.
    /// @param output Writable sample view borrowed for the duration of the call.
    /// @return Frames copied, or zero while waiting for prefill or when the queue is empty.
    /// @pre A nonempty view matches the node's channel count. Call from the I/O worker.
    std::size_t read(OFBlock output) noexcept;

    /// @brief Return the half-capacity prefill and drift target in frames.
    /// @pre Serialize with prepare().
    std::size_t target_frames() const noexcept { return ring_.capacity() / 2; }

    /// @brief Return the current number of queued frames.
    /// @pre Call from the external consumer or while both sides are idle.
    std::size_t available() const noexcept { return ring_.available(); }

    const std::chrono::microseconds io_period_;
    BlockPeriod period_{};
    FrameRing ring_;
    std::atomic<bool> backend_bound_{false};
    std::atomic<unsigned> graph_state_{0};
    std::atomic<bool> primed_{false};
    std::atomic<std::size_t> overflow_frames_{0};
};

} // namespace aereborn

#endif

===== plugins/matrix.cpp =====
#include "matrix.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstring>
namespace aereborn
{
namespace
{
void accumulate_steady(float *dst, const float *src, std::size_t frames, float gain) noexcept
{
    if (gain == 0.0f)
        return;
    for (std::size_t frame = 0; frame < frames; ++frame)
        dst[frame] += src[frame] * gain;
}

void accumulate_ramp(float *dst, const float *src, std::size_t frames, float coefficient, float start, float target,
                     double scale) noexcept
{
    const auto last = frames - 1;
    const auto begin = static_cast<double>(start);
    const auto end = static_cast<double>(target);
    for (std::size_t frame = 0; frame < last; ++frame)
    {
        // Scale after adding the weighted endpoints so exact crossings stay zero with fused multiply-add.
        const auto gain = coefficient * static_cast<float>((begin * (last - frame) + end * (frame + 1)) * scale);
        if (gain != 0.0f)
            dst[frame] += src[frame] * gain;
    }
    const auto end_gain = coefficient * target;
    if (end_gain != 0.0f)
        dst[last] += src[last] * end_gain;
}

std::size_t find_unity_source(const float *row, std::size_t input_channels, float target) noexcept
{
    std::size_t found = input_channels;
    for (std::size_t src_ch = 0; src_ch < input_channels; ++src_ch)
    {
        if (row[src_ch] == 0.0f)
            continue;
        if (found != input_channels || row[src_ch] * target != 1.0f)
            return input_channels;
        found = src_ch;
    }
    return found;
}
} // namespace

Matrix::Matrix(BandWidth rate, std::size_t channels, float gain)
    : NodeT({PortSpec(rate, channels, SampleFormat::F32P)}, {PortSpec(rate, channels, SampleFormat::F32P)}),
      target_(gain), gain_(gain)
{
    if (!inputs()[0].valid() || !std::isfinite(gain))
        throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM, "Invalid matrix specification");
    if (channels > coefficients_.max_size() / channels)
        throw Error(RetCode::TOOLARGE, "Channel matrix exceeds supported size");
    coefficients_.resize(channels * channels, 0.0f);
    for (std::size_t ch = 0; ch < channels; ++ch)
        coefficients_[ch * channels + ch] = 1.0f;
}
Matrix::Matrix(BandWidth rate, std::size_t input_channels, std::size_t output_channels, View<const float> coefficients,
               float gain)
    : NodeT({PortSpec(rate, input_channels, SampleFormat::F32P)},
            {PortSpec(rate, output_channels, SampleFormat::F32P)}),
      target_(gain), gain_(gain)
{
    if (!inputs()[0].valid() || !outputs()[0].valid() || !std::isfinite(gain))
        throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM, "Invalid matrix specification");
    if (output_channels > coefficients_.max_size() / input_channels)
        throw Error(RetCode::TOOLARGE, "Channel matrix exceeds supported size");
    if (coefficients.size() != input_channels * output_channels)
        throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM, "Channel matrix dimensions do not match coefficients");
    for (const auto value : coefficients)
        if (!std::isfinite(value))
            throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM, "Matrix coefficients must be finite");
    coefficients_.assign(coefficients.begin(), coefficients.end());
}
Matrix::Matrix(BandWidth rate, std::size_t input_channels, std::size_t output_channels,
               std::initializer_list<float> coefficients, float gain)
    : Matrix(rate, input_channels, output_channels, {coefficients.begin(), coefficients.size()}, gain)
{
}
void Matrix::set_gain(float gain)
{
    if (!std::isfinite(gain))
        throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM);
    target_.store(gain, std::memory_order_release);
}
float Matrix::gain() const noexcept
{
    return target_.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
}
void Matrix::prepare(BlockPeriod)
{
    reset();
}
void Matrix::reset() noexcept
{
    gain_ = target_.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
}
void Matrix::process(const ProcessContext &context) noexcept
{
    const auto output = context.outputs[0].f32p_view();
    const auto frames = output.frames();
    if (frames == 0)
        return;
    const auto target = target_.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    const bool steady = gain_ == target;
    const bool active = context.has_input(0) && !(steady && target == 0.0f);
    context.set_output_active(0, active);
    if (!active)
    {
        output.clear();
        gain_ = target;
        return;
    }
    const auto input = context.inputs[0].f32p_view();
    const auto scale = steady ? 0.0 : 1.0 / frames;
    const auto input_channels = input.channels();
    for (std::size_t dst_ch = 0; dst_ch < output.channels(); ++dst_ch)
    {
        auto *dst = output.channel(dst_ch);
        const auto *row = coefficients_.data() + dst_ch * input_channels;
        if (steady)
        {
            const auto unity_src = find_unity_source(row, input_channels, target);
            if (unity_src < input_channels)
            {
                std::memcpy(dst, input.channel(unity_src), frames * sizeof(float));
                continue;
            }
        }
        std::fill_n(dst, frames, 0.0f);
        for (std::size_t src_ch = 0; src_ch < input_channels; ++src_ch)
        {
            const auto coefficient = row[src_ch];
            if (coefficient == 0.0f)
                continue;
            const auto *src = input.channel(src_ch);
            if (steady)
                accumulate_steady(dst, src, frames, coefficient * target);
            else
                accumulate_ramp(dst, src, frames, coefficient, gain_, target, scale);
        }
    }
    gain_ = target;
}
} // namespace aereborn

===== plugins/matrix.h =====
#ifndef AEREBORN_PLUGINS_MATRIX_H
#define AEREBORN_PLUGINS_MATRIX_H
#include "core/graph.h"
#include <atomic>
#include <initializer_list>
#include <vector>
namespace aereborn
{
/// @brief Map F32P channels using a fixed matrix and a runtime-adjustable gain.
/// @note Matrix rows correspond to output channels and columns to input channels.
///       Both ports use the same sample rate.
class Matrix final : public NodeT<1, 1>
{
  public:
    /// @copydoc Node::type_name
    const char *type_name() const noexcept override { return "Matrix"; }

    /// @brief Construct an identity channel map with a gain applied to each channel.
    /// @param rate Sample rate of both ports.
    /// @param channels Channel count of both ports.
    /// @param gain Initial finite linear gain.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPARAM If the port specification is invalid or gain is not finite.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::TOOLARGE If the matrix exceeds the supported storage size.
    Matrix(BandWidth rate, std::size_t channels, float gain = 1.0f);

    /// @brief Construct a channel map from a row-major matrix.
    /// @param rate Sample rate of both ports.
    /// @param input_channels Number of columns.
    /// @param output_channels Number of rows.
    /// @param coefficients Finite coefficients in output-channel order, one row per output.
    /// @param gain Initial finite linear gain.
    /// @note The constructor copies the coefficients. Keep the view valid until it returns.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPARAM For invalid port specs, mismatched dimensions, or nonfinite coefficients or gain.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::TOOLARGE If the matrix exceeds the supported storage size.
    Matrix(BandWidth rate, std::size_t input_channels, std::size_t output_channels, View<const float> coefficients,
           float gain = 1.0f);

    /// @brief Construct a row-major channel map from an initializer list.
    /// @note Uses the same dimensions, validation, and copying rules as the view overload.
    Matrix(BandWidth rate, std::size_t input_channels, std::size_t output_channels,
           std::initializer_list<float> coefficients, float gain = 1.0f);

    /// @brief Set the requested linear gain.
    /// @param gain New finite gain target.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPARAM if the gain is not finite.
    /// @note Safe to call concurrently with process(). A nonempty block reaches its sampled target on its final frame.
    void set_gain(float gain);

    /// @brief Read the requested gain.
    /// @return The most recently set gain target.
    /// @note Safe to call concurrently with set_gain() and process().
    float gain() const noexcept;

    /// @brief Initialize the applied gain to the current target on the control thread.
    /// @param period Graph block period. Gain ramps use actual block sizes during processing.
    void prepare(BlockPeriod period) override;

    /// @copydoc Node::process
    /// @note Takes one gain target per nonempty block and ramps from the previously applied gain.
    void process(const ProcessContext &context) noexcept override;

    /// @brief Set the applied gain to the current target for the next block.
    /// @pre Serialize with process().
    void reset() noexcept override;

  private:
    std::vector<float> coefficients_;
    std::atomic<float> target_;
    float gain_;
};
} // namespace aereborn
#endif

===== plugins/mixer.cpp =====
#include "mixer.h"
namespace aereborn
{
namespace
{
PortSpec mixer_output(BandWidth rate, std::size_t channels, std::size_t inputs)
{
    const PortSpec spec(rate, channels, SampleFormat::F32P);
    if (!spec.valid() || inputs == 0)
        throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM, "Mixer needs valid ports and at least one input");
    if (inputs > Mixer::max_inputs)
        throw Error(RetCode::TOOLARGE);
    return spec;
}

constexpr std::uint64_t input_bit(std::size_t input) noexcept
{
    return std::uint64_t{1} << input;
}

std::uint64_t checked_bit(std::size_t input, std::size_t count)
{
    if (input >= count)
        throw Error(RetCode::EPORT);
    return input_bit(input);
}

void set_bit(std::atomic<std::uint64_t> &mask, std::uint64_t bit, bool enabled) noexcept
{
    if (enabled)
        mask.fetch_or(bit, std::memory_order_release);
    else
        mask.fetch_and(~bit, std::memory_order_release);
}

void accumulate_unity(OFBlock output, IFBlock input) noexcept
{
    const auto frames = output.frames();
    for (std::size_t ch = 0; ch < output.channels(); ++ch)
    {
        auto *dst = output.channel(ch);
        const auto *src = input.channel(ch);
        for (std::size_t frame = 0; frame < frames; ++frame)
            dst[frame] += src[frame];
    }
}

template <bool FadeOut>
void accumulate_ramp(OFBlock output, IFBlock input, double scale) noexcept
{
    const auto last = output.frames() - 1;
    for (std::size_t ch = 0; ch < output.channels(); ++ch)
    {
        auto *dst = output.channel(ch);
        const auto *src = input.channel(ch);
        for (std::size_t frame = 0; frame < last; ++frame)
        {
            const auto step = FadeOut ? last - frame : frame + 1;
            dst[frame] += src[frame] * static_cast<float>(step * scale);
        }
        // The final frame reaches unity on fade-in. At zero gain no source read is needed.
        if (!FadeOut)
            dst[last] += src[last];
    }
}
} // namespace

constexpr std::size_t Mixer::max_inputs;

Mixer::Mixer(BandWidth rate, std::size_t channels, std::size_t inputs)
    : output_(mixer_output(rate, channels, inputs)), inputs_(inputs, output_)
{
}
View<const PortSpec> Mixer::inputs() const noexcept
{
    return {inputs_.data(), inputs_.size()};
}
View<const PortSpec> Mixer::outputs() const noexcept
{
    return {&output_, 1};
}
void Mixer::set_muted(std::size_t input, bool muted)
{
    set_bit(muted_, checked_bit(input, inputs_.size()), muted);
}

bool Mixer::muted(std::size_t input) const
{
    const auto bit = checked_bit(input, inputs_.size());
    return (muted_.load(std::memory_order_acquire) & bit) != 0;
}

void Mixer::set_high_priority(std::size_t input, bool high)
{
    set_bit(high_, checked_bit(input, inputs_.size()), high);
}

bool Mixer::high_priority(std::size_t input) const
{
    const auto bit = checked_bit(input, inputs_.size());
    return (high_.load(std::memory_order_acquire) & bit) != 0;
}

bool Mixer::suppressed(std::size_t input) const
{
    const auto bit = checked_bit(input, inputs_.size());
    return (suppressed_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) & bit) != 0;
}

void Mixer::prepare(BlockPeriod)
{
    reset();
}

void Mixer::reset() noexcept
{
    was_suppressed_ = 0;
    suppressed_.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
}

void Mixer::process(const ProcessContext &context) noexcept
{
    const auto output = context.outputs[0].f32p_view();
    const auto frames = output.frames();
    if (frames == 0)
        return;

    const auto muted = muted_.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    const auto high = high_.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    const auto candidates = high & ~muted;
    const auto count = inputs_.size();
    std::uint64_t suppress = 0;
    if (candidates)
    {
        const auto normal = (~std::uint64_t{0} >> (max_inputs - count)) & ~high;
        if (normal)
        {
            for (std::size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i)
            {
                if ((candidates & input_bit(i)) && context.has_input(i))
                {
                    suppress = normal;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    const auto steady_suppressed = was_suppressed_ & suppress;
    const auto fading = was_suppressed_ ^ suppress;
    double scale = 0.0;
    output.clear();
    bool emitted = false;
    std::uint64_t suppressed = 0;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i)
    {
        const auto bit = input_bit(i);
        if (!context.has_input(i) || (muted & bit))
            continue;
        suppressed |= suppress & bit;
        if (steady_suppressed & bit)
            continue;

        emitted = true;
        const auto input = context.inputs[i].f32p_view();
        if (!(fading & bit))
        {
            accumulate_unity(output, input);
            continue;
        }
        if (scale == 0.0)
            scale = 1.0 / frames;
        if (suppress & bit)
            accumulate_ramp<true>(output, input, scale);
        else
            accumulate_ramp<false>(output, input, scale);
    }
    if (was_suppressed_ | suppress)
        suppressed_.store(suppressed, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    // Align offline normal inputs with the current suppression before they return.
    was_suppressed_ = suppress;
    context.set_output_active(0, emitted);
}
} // namespace aereborn

===== plugins/mixer.h =====
#ifndef AEREBORN_PLUGINS_MIXER_H
#define AEREBORN_PLUGINS_MIXER_H
#include "core/graph.h"
#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>
namespace aereborn
{
/// @brief Mix F32P inputs with optional high-priority suppression.
/// @note All ports have the same sample rate, channel count, and format.
/// @note Any active, unmuted high-priority input suppresses normal inputs over one block.
class Mixer final : public Node
{
  public:
    static constexpr std::size_t max_inputs = 64;

    /// @copydoc Node::type_name
    const char *type_name() const noexcept override { return "Mixer"; }

    /// @brief Construct a mixer with all inputs unmuted at normal priority.
    /// @param rate Sample rate of every port.
    /// @param channels Channel count of every port.
    /// @param inputs Number of input ports in [1, max_inputs].
    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPARAM If the port specification is invalid or inputs is zero.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::TOOLARGE if inputs exceeds max_inputs.
    Mixer(BandWidth rate, std::size_t channels, std::size_t inputs);

    /// @copydoc Node::inputs
    /// @note The view borrows port specifications from this mixer and remains valid until destruction.
    View<const PortSpec> inputs() const noexcept override;
    /// @copydoc Node::outputs
    /// @note The view borrows the port specification from this mixer and remains valid until destruction.
    View<const PortSpec> outputs() const noexcept override;

    /// @brief Set whether one input is muted.
    /// @param input Zero-based input port index.
    /// @param muted True to mute the input, false to unmute it.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPORT if the index is out of range.
    /// @note Safe to call concurrently with process(). Each nonempty block reads one mute mask.
    void set_muted(std::size_t input, bool muted);

    /// @brief Read the requested mute state of one input.
    /// @param input Zero-based input port index.
    /// @return The mute value.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPORT if the index is out of range.
    /// @note Safe to call concurrently with set_muted() and process().
    bool muted(std::size_t input) const;

    /// @brief Set whether an input has high priority.
    /// @param input Zero-based input port index.
    /// @param high True for high priority, false for normal priority.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPORT if the index is out of range.
    /// @note Safe to call concurrently with process(). High-priority inputs mix together.
    void set_high_priority(std::size_t input, bool high);

    /// @brief Read the requested priority of an input.
    /// @param input Zero-based input port index.
    /// @return True if the input has high priority.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPORT if the index is out of range.
    /// @note Safe to call concurrently with set_high_priority() and process().
    bool high_priority(std::size_t input) const;

    /// @brief Report whether an active, unmuted input was suppressed in the latest nonempty block.
    /// @param input Zero-based input port index.
    /// @return True during the fade-out and steady suppression blocks.
    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPORT if the index is out of range.
    /// @note Safe to call concurrently with process().
    bool suppressed(std::size_t input) const;

    /// @brief Initialize runtime suppression state on the control thread.
    void prepare(BlockPeriod period) override;

    /// @copydoc Node::process
    /// @note Each nonempty block reads the mute and priority masks once.
    void process(const ProcessContext &context) noexcept override;

    /// @brief Clear runtime suppression while keeping the requested mute and priority settings.
    /// @pre Serialize with process().
    void reset() noexcept override;

  private:
    const PortSpec output_;
    const std::vector<PortSpec> inputs_;
    std::atomic<std::uint64_t> muted_{0};
    std::atomic<std::uint64_t> high_{0};
    std::atomic<std::uint64_t> suppressed_{0};
    std::uint64_t was_suppressed_ = 0;
};
} // namespace aereborn
#endif

===== examples/clocked_graph.cpp =====
#include "core/engine.h"
#include "plugins/matrix.h"
#include "plugins/mixer.h"
#include "tests/support/tone.h"
#include <atomic>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <iostream>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <thread>
namespace
{
using namespace aereborn;
class Meter final : public NodeT<1, 0>
{
  public:
    explicit Meter(std::atomic<float> &level)
        : NodeT({PortSpec(BandWidth::Full, 1, SampleFormat::F32P)}, {}), level_(level)
    {
    }
    void process(const ProcessContext &context) noexcept override
    {
        const auto input = context.inputs[0].f32p_view();
        float level = 0;
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < input.frames(); ++i)
            level = std::max(level, std::abs(input.channel(0)[i]));
        level_.store(level, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    }

  private:
    std::atomic<float> &level_;
};
} // namespace
int main()
{
    try
    {
        std::atomic<float> level{0};
        Engine engine(BlockPeriod::INR_05MS);
        const auto tone = engine.emplace_node<Tone>(BandWidth::Full, 1);
        const auto mix = engine.emplace_node<Mixer>(BandWidth::Full, 1, 1);
        const auto matrix = engine.emplace_node<Matrix>(BandWidth::Full, 1);
        const auto sink = engine.emplace_node<Meter>(level);
        engine.connect(tone, mix);
        engine.connect(mix, matrix);
        engine.connect(matrix, sink);
        auto *gain = engine.node<Matrix>(matrix);
        engine.commit();
        engine.start();
        const auto wait = [&]
        {
            const auto target = engine.tick_position() + 4;
            const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(3);
            while (engine.tick_position() < target)
            {
                if (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() > deadline)
                    throw std::runtime_error("Audio timeout");
                std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(1));
            }
            std::cout << "tick=" << engine.tick_position() << " peak=" << level.load() << '\n';
        };
        wait();
        gain->set_gain(0.5f);
        wait();
        engine.stop();
    }
    catch (const std::exception &error)
    {
        std::cerr << error.what() << '\n';
        return 1;
    }
}
