===== backend/usr_playback.h =====
#ifndef AEREBORN_BACKEND_USR_PLAYBACK_H
#define AEREBORN_BACKEND_USR_PLAYBACK_H

#include "backend.h"
#include <functional>

namespace aereborn
{

/// @brief Deliver fixed interleaved PCM batches on a worker thread as graph data becomes available.
/// @note The callback borrows the block for the duration of the call and must return without throwing.
/// @note Serialize control operations outside the callback. Stop waits for the callback and drains queued audio.
class UsrPlayback final : public ActiveBackend
{
  public:
    /// @copydoc Backend::type_name
    const char *type_name() const noexcept override { return "UsrPlayback"; }

    using Callback = std::function<void(IBBlock)>;

    /// @throws Error RetCode::EPARAM if spec is not valid interleaved PCM or callback is empty.
    UsrPlayback(PortSpec spec, Callback callback);
    ~UsrPlayback() override { unbind(); }

    using ActiveBackend::bind;
    void bind(Capture &) = delete;

  private:
    Result<PortSpec> on_open(bool capture, PortSpec logical, std::chrono::microseconds target_period) override;
    RetCode on_write(IBBlock input) noexcept override;

    const PortSpec spec_;
    const Callback callback_;
};

} // namespace aereborn

#endif

===== backend/wasapi_backend.cpp =====
#include "wasapi_backend.h"

#if !WINDOWS_OS_ENVIRONMENT
#error "WASAPI requires a Windows Vista or later SDK target (_WIN32_WINNT >= 0x0600)"
#endif

#ifndef NOMINMAX
#define NOMINMAX
#endif
#include <windows.h>
#include <initguid.h>
#include <functiondiscoverykeys_devpkey.h>
#include <audioclient.h>
#include <mmdeviceapi.h>
#include <propidl.h>
#include <propsys.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cassert>
#include <cstring>
#include <cwchar>
#include <limits>
#include <memory>
#include <utility>

namespace aereborn
{
namespace
{

struct ReleaseInterface
{
    void operator()(IUnknown *ptr) const noexcept { ptr->Release(); }
};

template <class T> using Interface = std::unique_ptr<T, ReleaseInterface>;

struct FreeComBuffer
{
    void operator()(void *ptr) const noexcept { CoTaskMemFree(ptr); }
};

struct CloseEvent
{
    void operator()(void *event) const noexcept { CloseHandle(event); }
};

class ComApartment
{
  public:
    explicit ComApartment(DWORD mode) noexcept : result(CoInitializeEx(nullptr, mode)) {}
    ~ComApartment() { if (SUCCEEDED(result) && active) CoUninitialize(); }
    HRESULT result;
    bool active = true;
};

struct PropertyValue
{
    PROPVARIANT value{};
    ~PropertyValue() { PropVariantClear(&value); }
};

RetCode record_failure(WasapiBackend::Failure &failure, const char *operation, HRESULT error) noexcept
{
    failure = {operation, static_cast<std::int32_t>(error)};
    if (error == AUDCLNT_E_WRONG_ENDPOINT_TYPE || error == E_INVALIDARG ||
        error == HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_NO_UNICODE_TRANSLATION))
        return RetCode::EPARAM;
    if (error == AUDCLNT_E_UNSUPPORTED_FORMAT)
        return RetCode::INVFMT;
    if (error == AUDCLNT_E_DEVICE_INVALIDATED || error == HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_NOT_FOUND))
        return RetCode::DEVLOST;
    if (error == E_OUTOFMEMORY)
        return RetCode::TOOLARGE;
    return RetCode::IOFAILED;
}

Result<std::wstring> device_name(const std::string &name, WasapiBackend::Failure &failure)
{
    if (name.empty() || name == "default")
        return std::wstring{};
    if (name.size() > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()))
        return RetCode::TOOLARGE;
    const auto bytes = static_cast<int>(name.size());
    const auto size = MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, MB_ERR_INVALID_CHARS, name.data(), bytes, nullptr, 0);
    if (!size)
        return record_failure(failure, "MultiByteToWideChar", HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(GetLastError()));
    std::wstring wide(static_cast<std::size_t>(size), L'\0');
    if (!MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, MB_ERR_INVALID_CHARS, name.data(), bytes, &wide[0], size))
        return record_failure(failure, "MultiByteToWideChar", HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(GetLastError()));
    return wide;
}

Result<Interface<IMMDeviceEnumerator>> make_enumerator(WasapiBackend::Failure &failure) noexcept
{
    IMMDeviceEnumerator *enumerator_ptr = nullptr;
    const auto rc = CoCreateInstance(__uuidof(MMDeviceEnumerator), nullptr, CLSCTX_ALL,
                                     IID_PPV_ARGS(&enumerator_ptr));
    if (FAILED(rc))
        return record_failure(failure, "CoCreateInstance(MMDeviceEnumerator)", rc);
    return Interface<IMMDeviceEnumerator>(enumerator_ptr);
}

Result<Interface<IMMDevice>> find_friendly_name(IMMDeviceEnumerator &enumerator, const std::wstring &name,
                                                EDataFlow direction, WasapiBackend::Failure &failure)
{
    IMMDeviceCollection *devices_ptr = nullptr;
    auto rc = enumerator.EnumAudioEndpoints(direction, DEVICE_STATE_ACTIVE, &devices_ptr);
    if (FAILED(rc))
        return record_failure(failure, "EnumAudioEndpoints", rc);
    const Interface<IMMDeviceCollection> devices(devices_ptr);
    UINT count = 0;
    rc = devices->GetCount(&count);
    if (FAILED(rc))
        return record_failure(failure, "IMMDeviceCollection::GetCount", rc);

    Interface<IMMDevice> match;
    bool exact = false;
    bool ambiguous = false;
    for (UINT i = 0; i < count; ++i)
    {
        IMMDevice *device_ptr = nullptr;
        rc = devices->Item(i, &device_ptr);
        if (FAILED(rc))
            continue;
        Interface<IMMDevice> device(device_ptr);
        IPropertyStore *properties_ptr = nullptr;
        rc = device->OpenPropertyStore(STGM_READ, &properties_ptr);
        if (FAILED(rc))
            continue;
        const Interface<IPropertyStore> properties(properties_ptr);
        PropertyValue friendly;
        rc = properties->GetValue(PKEY_Device_FriendlyName, &friendly.value);
        if (FAILED(rc) || friendly.value.vt != VT_LPWSTR || !friendly.value.pwszVal)
            continue;

        const auto *value = friendly.value.pwszVal;
        const auto exact_name = name == value;
        if (!exact_name && (exact || !std::wcsstr(value, name.c_str())))
            continue;

        if (match && exact_name == exact)
        {
            ambiguous = true;
            if (exact)
                break;
            continue;
        }
        match = std::move(device);
        exact = exact_name;
        ambiguous = false;
    }
    if (ambiguous)
        return record_failure(failure, "Ambiguous WASAPI friendly name", E_INVALIDARG);
    if (match)
        return match;
    return record_failure(failure, "WASAPI friendly name", HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_NOT_FOUND));
}

Result<Interface<IMMDevice>> resolve_device(IMMDeviceEnumerator &enumerator, const std::wstring &name,
                                            EDataFlow direction, WasapiBackend::Failure &failure)
{
    IMMDevice *device_ptr = nullptr;
    const auto use_default = name.empty();
    auto rc = use_default ? enumerator.GetDefaultAudioEndpoint(direction, eConsole, &device_ptr)
                          : enumerator.GetDevice(name.c_str(), &device_ptr);
    if (FAILED(rc))
    {
        if (!use_default && rc == HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(ERROR_NOT_FOUND))
            return find_friendly_name(enumerator, name, direction, failure);
        return record_failure(failure, use_default ? "GetDefaultAudioEndpoint" : "GetDevice", rc);
    }

    Interface<IMMDevice> device(device_ptr);
    if (use_default)
        return device;
    IMMEndpoint *endpoint_ptr = nullptr;
    rc = device->QueryInterface(IID_PPV_ARGS(&endpoint_ptr));
    if (FAILED(rc))
        return record_failure(failure, "QueryInterface(IMMEndpoint)", rc);
    const Interface<IMMEndpoint> endpoint(endpoint_ptr);
    EDataFlow flow;
    rc = endpoint->GetDataFlow(&flow);
    if (FAILED(rc))
        return record_failure(failure, "IMMEndpoint::GetDataFlow", rc);
    if (flow != direction)
        return record_failure(failure, "WASAPI endpoint direction", AUDCLNT_E_WRONG_ENDPOINT_TYPE);
    return device;
}

Result<Interface<IAudioClient>> activate(IMMDevice &device, WasapiBackend::Failure &failure) noexcept
{
    DWORD state = 0;
    auto rc = device.GetState(&state);
    if (FAILED(rc))
        return record_failure(failure, "IMMDevice::GetState", rc);
    if (state != DEVICE_STATE_ACTIVE)
        return record_failure(failure, "IMMDevice::GetState", AUDCLNT_E_DEVICE_INVALIDATED);

    IAudioClient *client_ptr = nullptr;
    rc = device.Activate(__uuidof(IAudioClient), CLSCTX_ALL, nullptr, reinterpret_cast<void **>(&client_ptr));
    if (FAILED(rc))
        return record_failure(failure, "IMMDevice::Activate(IAudioClient)", rc);
    return Interface<IAudioClient>(client_ptr);
}

SampleFormat sample_format(const WAVEFORMATEX &format) noexcept
{
    auto tag = format.wFormatTag;
    if (tag == WAVE_FORMAT_EXTENSIBLE)
    {
        if (format.cbSize < sizeof(WAVEFORMATEXTENSIBLE) - sizeof(WAVEFORMATEX))
            return {};
        const auto &ext = reinterpret_cast<const WAVEFORMATEXTENSIBLE &>(format);
        constexpr GUID pcm{1, 0, 0x0010, {0x80, 0, 0, 0xaa, 0, 0x38, 0x9b, 0x71}};
        constexpr GUID floating{3, 0, 0x0010, {0x80, 0, 0, 0xaa, 0, 0x38, 0x9b, 0x71}};
        if (IsEqualGUID(ext.SubFormat, pcm))
            tag = WAVE_FORMAT_PCM;
        else if (IsEqualGUID(ext.SubFormat, floating))
            tag = WAVE_FORMAT_IEEE_FLOAT;
        else
            return {};
    }
    if (tag == WAVE_FORMAT_IEEE_FLOAT && format.wBitsPerSample == 32)
        return SampleFormat::F32LE;
    if (tag == WAVE_FORMAT_PCM)
    {
        switch (format.wBitsPerSample)
        {
        case 16: return SampleFormat::S16LE;
        case 24: return SampleFormat::S24_3LE;
        case 32: return SampleFormat::S32LE;
        }
    }
    return {};
}

Result<UINT32> initialize(IAudioClient &client, const WAVEFORMATEX &format, WasapiBackend::Failure &failure) noexcept
{
    auto rc = client.Initialize(AUDCLNT_SHAREMODE_SHARED, AUDCLNT_STREAMFLAGS_EVENTCALLBACK,
                                0, 0, &format, nullptr);
    if (FAILED(rc))
        return record_failure(failure, "IAudioClient::Initialize", rc);
    UINT32 capacity = 0;
    rc = client.GetBufferSize(&capacity);
    if (FAILED(rc))
        return record_failure(failure, "IAudioClient::GetBufferSize", rc);
    return capacity;
}

} // namespace

Result<PortSpec> WasapiBackend::on_open(bool capture, PortSpec logical, std::chrono::microseconds period)
{
    failure_ = {};
    ComApartment com(COINIT_APARTMENTTHREADED);
    if (FAILED(com.result) && com.result != RPC_E_CHANGED_MODE)
        return record_failure(failure_, "CoInitializeEx", com.result);

    const auto name = device_name(device_, failure_);
    if (!name)
        return name.ret_code();
    const auto enumerator = make_enumerator(failure_);
    if (!enumerator)
        return enumerator.ret_code();
    const auto device = resolve_device(*enumerator.value(), name.value(), capture ? eCapture : eRender, failure_);
    if (!device)
        return device.ret_code();
    LPWSTR id_ptr = nullptr;
    auto rc = device.value()->GetId(&id_ptr);
    if (FAILED(rc))
        return record_failure(failure_, "IMMDevice::GetId", rc);
    const std::unique_ptr<wchar_t, FreeComBuffer> id(id_ptr);

    const auto probe = activate(*device.value(), failure_);
    if (!probe)
        return probe.ret_code();
    WAVEFORMATEX *format_ptr = nullptr;
    rc = probe.value()->GetMixFormat(&format_ptr);
    if (FAILED(rc))
        return record_failure(failure_, "IAudioClient::GetMixFormat", rc);
    const std::unique_ptr<WAVEFORMATEX, FreeComBuffer> format(format_ptr);
    const PortSpec spec{static_cast<BandWidth>(format->nSamplesPerSec), format->nChannels, sample_format(*format)};
    if (!valid_pcm_spec(spec) || logical.channels != spec.channels ||
        format->nBlockAlign != spec.channels * sample_bytes(spec.format) ||
        sizeof(*format) + format->cbSize > format_.size())
        return RetCode::INVFMT;
    const auto budget = frames_in_period(spec.bandwidth, period);
    if (!budget)
        return budget.ret_code();

    const auto initialized = initialize(*probe.value(), *format, failure_);
    if (!initialized)
        return initialized.ret_code();
    const auto capacity = initialized.value();
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> packet;
    if (capture)
    {
        if (capacity > packet.max_size() / format->nBlockAlign)
            return RetCode::TOOLARGE;
        packet.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(capacity) * format->nBlockAlign);
    }

    std::unique_ptr<void, CloseEvent> ready(CreateEventW(nullptr, FALSE, FALSE, nullptr));
    if (!ready)
        return record_failure(failure_, "CreateEventW(ready)", HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(GetLastError()));
    std::unique_ptr<void, CloseEvent> cancelled(CreateEventW(nullptr, TRUE, FALSE, nullptr));
    if (!cancelled)
        return record_failure(failure_, "CreateEventW(cancelled)", HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(GetLastError()));

    static_assert(sizeof(WAVEFORMATEXTENSIBLE) == 40, "Unexpected WAVEFORMATEXTENSIBLE layout");
    format_.fill(0);
    std::memcpy(format_.data(), format.get(), sizeof(*format) + format->cbSize);
    device_id_.assign(id.get());
    packet_ = std::move(packet);
    ready_ = ready.release();
    cancelled_ = cancelled.release();
    set_device_max_frames(std::min<std::size_t>(budget.value(), capacity));
    return spec;
}

void WasapiBackend::on_close() noexcept
{
    if (!ready_)
        return;
    CloseHandle(cancelled_);
    CloseHandle(ready_);
    cancelled_ = ready_ = nullptr;
    device_id_.clear();
    std::vector<std::uint8_t>().swap(packet_);
}

RetCode WasapiBackend::on_start() noexcept
{
    failure_ = {};
    if (!ResetEvent(ready_) || !ResetEvent(cancelled_))
        return record_failure(failure_, "ResetEvent", HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(GetLastError()));
    const auto source = is_capture();
    ComApartment com(COINIT_MULTITHREADED);
    if (FAILED(com.result))
        return record_failure(failure_, "CoInitializeEx", com.result);
    const auto enumerator = make_enumerator(failure_);
    if (!enumerator)
        return enumerator.ret_code();
    IMMDevice *device_ptr = nullptr;
    auto rc = enumerator.value()->GetDevice(device_id_.c_str(), &device_ptr);
    if (FAILED(rc))
        return record_failure(failure_, "GetDevice", rc);
    const Interface<IMMDevice> device(device_ptr);
    auto activated = activate(*device, failure_);
    if (!activated)
        return activated.ret_code();
    auto client = std::move(activated).value();
    WAVEFORMATEXTENSIBLE format;
    std::memcpy(&format, format_.data(), sizeof(format));
    const auto initialized = initialize(*client, format.Format, failure_);
    if (!initialized)
        return initialized.ret_code();
    const auto capacity = initialized.value();
    if (source && capacity > packet_.size() / format.Format.nBlockAlign)
        return RetCode::TOOLARGE;
    rc = client->SetEventHandle(ready_);
    if (FAILED(rc))
        return record_failure(failure_, "IAudioClient::SetEventHandle", rc);

    IAudioCaptureClient *capture = nullptr;
    IAudioRenderClient *render = nullptr;
    rc = source ? client->GetService(IID_PPV_ARGS(&capture)) : client->GetService(IID_PPV_ARGS(&render));
    if (FAILED(rc))
        return record_failure(failure_, "IAudioClient::GetService", rc);
    Interface<IAudioCaptureClient> capture_service(capture);
    Interface<IAudioRenderClient> render_service(render);
    if (render_service)
    {
        // Prime the endpoint buffer before the audio engine starts consuming it.
        const auto primed = write(*render_service, capacity, {});
        if (!primed)
            return primed;
    }
    rc = client->Start();
    if (FAILED(rc))
        return record_failure(failure_, "IAudioClient::Start", rc);

    packet_offset_ = packet_size_ = 0;
    capacity_ = capacity;
    client_ = client.release();
    capture_ = capture_service.release();
    render_ = render_service.release();
    com.active = false;
    return {};
}

void WasapiBackend::on_stop() noexcept
{
    client_->Stop();
    if (capture_)
        capture_->Release();
    else
        render_->Release();
    client_->Release();
    capture_ = nullptr;
    render_ = nullptr;
    client_ = nullptr;
    CoUninitialize();
}

void WasapiBackend::on_cancel() noexcept
{
    SetEvent(cancelled_);
}

RetCode WasapiBackend::wait() noexcept
{
    const HANDLE handles[] = {cancelled_, ready_};
    const auto timeout = remaining_io_ms();
    if (!timeout)
        return RetCode::TIMEOUT;
    switch (WaitForMultipleObjects(2, handles, FALSE, static_cast<DWORD>(timeout)))
    {
    case WAIT_OBJECT_0: return RetCode::CANCELLED;
    case WAIT_OBJECT_0 + 1: return {};
    case WAIT_TIMEOUT: return RetCode::TIMEOUT;
    case WAIT_FAILED:
        return record_failure(failure_, "WaitForMultipleObjects", HRESULT_FROM_WIN32(GetLastError()));
    default: return RetCode::IOFAILED;
    }
}

Result<std::size_t> WasapiBackend::on_read(OBBlock output) noexcept
{
    const auto width = output.spec().channels * sample_bytes(output.spec().format);
    const auto dest = output.byte_view();
    while (packet_offset_ == packet_size_)
    {
        BYTE *bytes = nullptr;
        UINT32 frames = 0;
        DWORD flags = 0;
        auto rc = capture_->GetBuffer(&bytes, &frames, &flags, nullptr, nullptr);
        if (FAILED(rc))
            return record_failure(failure_, "IAudioCaptureClient::GetBuffer", rc);
        if (!frames)
        {
            // No packet needs releasing when GetBuffer reports zero frames.
            const auto ready = wait();
            if (!ready)
                return ready;
            continue;
        }

        assert(frames <= capacity_);
        const auto received = std::min<std::size_t>(frames, output.frames());
        const auto copied = received * width;
        const auto remaining = (frames - received) * width;
        if (flags & AUDCLNT_BUFFERFLAGS_SILENT)
        {
            std::memset(dest.data(), 0, copied);
            if (remaining)
                std::memset(packet_.data(), 0, remaining);
        }
        else
        {
            std::memcpy(dest.data(), bytes, copied);
            if (remaining)
                std::memcpy(packet_.data(), bytes + copied, remaining);
        }
        rc = capture_->ReleaseBuffer(frames);
        if (FAILED(rc))
            return record_failure(failure_, "IAudioCaptureClient::ReleaseBuffer", rc);
        if (remaining)
        {
            packet_offset_ = 0;
            packet_size_ = remaining;
        }
        return received;
    }

    const auto size = std::min(dest.size(), packet_size_ - packet_offset_);
    std::memcpy(dest.data(), packet_.data() + packet_offset_, size);
    packet_offset_ += size;
    return size / width;
}

Result<std::size_t> WasapiBackend::on_wait_writable() noexcept
{
    for (;;)
    {
        UINT32 used = 0;
        const auto rc = client_->GetCurrentPadding(&used);
        if (FAILED(rc))
            return record_failure(failure_, "IAudioClient::GetCurrentPadding", rc);
        if (used < capacity_)
            return std::min<std::size_t>(capacity_ - used, device_max_frames());
        const auto ready = wait();
        if (!ready)
            return ready;
    }
}

RetCode WasapiBackend::on_write(IBBlock input) noexcept
{
    return write(*render_, static_cast<UINT32>(input.frames()), input.byte_view());
}

RetCode WasapiBackend::on_write_silence(OBBlock scratch) noexcept
{
    return write(*render_, static_cast<UINT32>(scratch.frames()), {});
}

RetCode WasapiBackend::write(IAudioRenderClient &render, std::uint32_t frames, View<const std::uint8_t> bytes) noexcept
{
    BYTE *dest = nullptr;
    auto rc = render.GetBuffer(frames, &dest);
    if (FAILED(rc))
        return record_failure(failure_, "IAudioRenderClient::GetBuffer", rc);
    const auto silent = bytes.empty();
    if (!silent)
        std::memcpy(dest, bytes.data(), bytes.size());
    rc = render.ReleaseBuffer(frames, silent ? AUDCLNT_BUFFERFLAGS_SILENT : 0);
    return FAILED(rc) ? record_failure(failure_, "IAudioRenderClient::ReleaseBuffer", rc) : RetCode{};
}

WasapiBackend::WasapiBackend(std::string device, bool fail_on_timeout)
    : ActiveBackend(true, fail_on_timeout), device_(std::move(device))
{
}

} // namespace aereborn

===== backend/wasapi_backend.h =====
#ifndef AEREBORN_BACKEND_WASAPI_BACKEND_H
#define AEREBORN_BACKEND_WASAPI_BACKEND_H

#include "audio_types.h"
#include "backend.h"

#if WINDOWS_OS_ENVIRONMENT

struct IAudioClient;
struct IAudioCaptureClient;
struct IAudioRenderClient;

namespace aereborn

/// @brief Event-driven WASAPI backend for a Windows Vista or later SDK target.
class WasapiBackend final : public ActiveBackend
{
  public:
    /// @copydoc Backend::type_name
    const char *type_name() const noexcept override
    {
        return bound() ? (is_capture() ? "WasapiCapture" : "WasapiPlayback") : "WasapiBackend";
    }

    /// @brief Native failure details. operation is null when no native failure was recorded.
    struct Failure
    {
        const char *operation = nullptr;
        std::int32_t hresult = 0;
    };

    /// @brief Construct a backend whose direction is selected when it binds.
    /// @param device UTF-8 endpoint ID or friendly name. Empty or "default" selects the system default.
    /// @param fail_on_timeout Stop the worker when PCM I/O times out.
    /// @pre The device name contains no embedded NUL bytes.
    explicit WasapiBackend(std::string device, bool fail_on_timeout = true);
    ~WasapiBackend() override { unbind(); }

    /// @brief Return the latest native failure since binding or starting, or an empty value while running.
    /// @note Call on the control thread, serialized with bind(), start(), stop(), and unbind().
    Failure last_failure() const noexcept { return running() ? Failure{} : failure_; }

  private:
    Result<PortSpec> on_open(bool capture, PortSpec logical, std::chrono::microseconds period) override;
    RetCode on_start() noexcept override;
    void on_stop() noexcept override;
    void on_cancel() noexcept override;
    void on_close() noexcept override;

    Result<std::size_t> on_read(OBBlock output) noexcept override;
    Result<std::size_t> on_wait_writable() noexcept override;
    RetCode on_write(IBBlock input) noexcept override;
    RetCode on_write_silence(OBBlock scratch) noexcept override;
    RetCode wait() noexcept;
    RetCode write(IAudioRenderClient &render, std::uint32_t frames, View<const std::uint8_t> bytes) noexcept;

    Failure failure_;
    std::string device_;
    std::wstring device_id_;
    std::array<std::uint8_t, 40> format_{};
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> packet_;
    std::size_t packet_offset_ = 0;
    std::size_t packet_size_ = 0;
    std::uint32_t capacity_ = 0;
    void *ready_ = nullptr;
    void *cancelled_ = nullptr;
    IAudioClient *client_ = nullptr;
    IAudioCaptureClient *capture_ = nullptr;
    IAudioRenderClient *render_ = nullptr;
};

} // namespace aereborn
#endif
#endif

===== backend/wav_backend.cpp =====
#include "wav_backend.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <limits>
#include <utility>

namespace aereborn
{
namespace
{

constexpr auto segment_number_width = std::numeric_limits<std::uint32_t>::digits10 + 1;

} // namespace

WavCapture::WavCapture(std::string path, bool loop)
    : ActiveBackend(false), path_(std::move(path)), loop_(loop)
{
}

WavCapture::WavCapture(std::string path, Repeat repeats)
    : ActiveBackend(false), path_(std::move(path)), repeats_(repeats.count)
{
}

Result<PortSpec> WavCapture::probe() noexcept
{
    const auto status = file_.status();
    const auto opened = status == RetCode::ESTATE ? file_.open_read(path_) : status;
    if (!opened)
        return opened;
    return file_.spec();
}

Result<PortSpec> WavCapture::on_open(bool capture, PortSpec, std::chrono::microseconds period)
{
    if (!capture)
        return RetCode::EPARAM;
    const auto input = probe();
    if (!input)
        return input.ret_code();
    const auto spec = input.value();
    const auto count = frames_in_period(spec.bandwidth, period);
    if (!count)
        return count.ret_code();

    repeats_left_ = repeats_;
    set_device_max_frames(count.value());
    return spec;
}

void WavCapture::on_close() noexcept
{
    file_.close();
}

Result<std::size_t> WavCapture::on_read(OBBlock output) noexcept
{
    const auto spec = output.spec();
    const auto width = spec.channels * sample_bytes(spec.format);
    auto *dest = output.byte_view().data();
    std::size_t frames = 0;
    while (frames < output.frames())
    {
        if (file_.tell() == file_.frame_count())
        {
            if (!file_.frame_count() || (!loop_ && !repeats_left_))
                break;
            const auto code = file_.seek(0);
            if (!code)
                return code;
            if (!loop_)
                --repeats_left_;
        }
        const auto read = file_.read({dest + frames * width, spec, output.frames() - frames});
        if (!read)
            return read;
        frames += read.value();
    }
    if (!frames)
        return RetCode::EOS;
    return frames;
}

WavPlayback::WavPlayback(std::string path, SampleFormat format)
    : WavPlayback(std::move(path), format, std::chrono::seconds::zero())
{
}

WavPlayback::WavPlayback(std::string path, SampleFormat format, std::chrono::seconds segment_duration,
                         WavFile::WriteMode write_mode)
    : ActiveBackend(false), path_(std::move(path)), format_(format), segment_duration_(segment_duration),
      write_mode_(write_mode)
{
    if (segment_duration_.count() < 0)
        throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM);
    if (!WavFile::supports_write_format(format_))
        throw Error(RetCode::INVFMT);
}

Result<PortSpec> WavPlayback::on_open(bool capture, PortSpec logical, std::chrono::microseconds period)
{
    if (capture)
        return RetCode::EPARAM;
    const auto count = frames_in_period(logical.bandwidth, period);
    if (!count)
        return count.ret_code();
    logical.format = format_;
    if (segment_duration_.count())
    {
        const auto limit = WavFile::max_write_frames(logical);
        if (!limit)
            return limit.ret_code();
        const auto hz = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(logical.bandwidth);
        const auto seconds = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(segment_duration_.count());
        if (seconds > limit.value() / hz)
            return RetCode::TOOLARGE;
        segment_frames_ = static_cast<std::size_t>(seconds * hz);

        segment_path_ = path_;
        const auto separator = path_.find_last_of("/\\");
        const auto name_start = separator == std::string::npos ? 0 : separator + 1;
        if (name_start == path_.size())
            return RetCode::EPARAM;
        auto extension = path_.find_last_of('.');
        if (extension == std::string::npos || extension <= name_start)
        {
            extension = segment_path_.size();
            segment_path_ += ".wav";
        }
        segment_path_.insert(extension, 1, '-');
        segment_number_offset_ = extension + 1;
        segment_path_.insert(segment_number_offset_, segment_number_width, '0');
        segment_index_ = 0;
    }
    set_device_max_frames(count.value());
    return logical;
}

RetCode WavPlayback::on_bind_ready(PortSpec spec) noexcept
{
    // Create the file only after allocation succeeds so the same path is available for a retry.
    return open_file(spec);
}

RetCode WavPlayback::on_start() noexcept
{
    const auto state = file_.status();
    return state == RetCode::ESTATE ? open_file(device_spec()) : state;
}

void WavPlayback::on_stop() noexcept
{
    const auto flushed = file_.flush();
    if (flushed != RetCode::ESTATE)
        record_io_error(flushed);
}

RetCode WavPlayback::open_file(PortSpec spec) noexcept
{
    if (!segment_frames_)
        return file_.open_write(path_, spec, write_mode_);
    for (;;)
    {
        if (segment_index_ == std::numeric_limits<std::uint32_t>::max())
            return RetCode::TOOLARGE;
        auto index = ++segment_index_;
        for (auto pos = segment_number_offset_ + segment_number_width; pos != segment_number_offset_; index /= 10)
            segment_path_[--pos] = static_cast<char>('0' + index % 10);
        const auto opened = file_.open_write(segment_path_, spec, WavFile::WriteMode::Exclusive);
        if (opened != RetCode::BUSY)
            return opened;
    }
}

void WavPlayback::on_close() noexcept
{
    const auto result = file_.close();
    if (last_error())
        record_io_error(result);
}

RetCode WavPlayback::on_write(IBBlock input) noexcept
{
    if (!segment_frames_)
        return file_.write(input);

    const auto spec = input.spec();
    const auto width = spec.channels * sample_bytes(spec.format);
    const auto *bytes = input.byte_view().data();
    std::size_t written = 0;
    while (written < input.frames())
    {
        if (file_.frame_count() == segment_frames_)
        {
            const auto closed = file_.close();
            if (!closed)
                return closed;
            const auto opened = open_file(spec);
            if (!opened)
                return opened;
        }
        const auto count = std::min(input.frames() - written, segment_frames_ - file_.frame_count());
        const auto code = file_.write({bytes + written * width, spec, count});
        if (!code)
            return code;
        written += count;
    }
    return {};
}

} // namespace aereborn

===== backend/wav_backend.h =====
#ifndef AEREBORN_BACKEND_WAV_BACKEND_H
#define AEREBORN_BACKEND_WAV_BACKEND_H

#include "backend.h"
#include "wav_file.h"
#include <string>

namespace aereborn
{

/// @brief Graph-clocked RIFF/WAVE source. Reports RetCode::EOS when all passes have been read.
/// @note Unsigned 8-bit PCM and 64-bit float files are exposed as F32LE.
/// @note Restart resumes at the next file frame to read. Rebinding opens the file from the beginning.
class WavCapture final : public ActiveBackend
{
  public:
    /// @copydoc Backend::type_name
    const char *type_name() const noexcept override { return "WavCapture"; }

    /// @brief Borrow the configured WAV input path.
    const std::string &path() const noexcept { return path_; }

    /// @brief Number of additional passes after the first one.
    struct Repeat
    {
        std::size_t count;
    };

    /// @brief Play once or loop indefinitely.
    explicit WavCapture(std::string path, bool loop);
    /// @brief Play once and then repeat the requested number of times.
    explicit WavCapture(std::string path, Repeat repeats);
    ~WavCapture() override { unbind(); }

    /// @brief Open the input to obtain its PCM specification. The next bind reuses the reader.
    /// @pre Call on the control side before binding.
    Result<PortSpec> probe() noexcept;

    using ActiveBackend::bind;
    void bind(Playback &) = delete;

  private:
    Result<PortSpec> on_open(bool capture, PortSpec logical, std::chrono::microseconds period) override;
    void on_close() noexcept override;
    Result<std::size_t> on_read(OBBlock output) noexcept override;

    std::string path_;
    bool loop_ = false;
    std::size_t repeats_ = 0;
    WavFile file_;
    std::size_t repeats_left_ = 0;
};

/// @brief Graph-clocked RIFF/WAVE sink supporting S16LE, S24_3LE, S32LE and F32LE.
/// @note Stop drains and flushes the audio. Unbind and rotation finalize the RIFF and data lengths.
class WavPlayback final : public ActiveBackend
{
  public:
    /// @copydoc Backend::type_name
    const char *type_name() const noexcept override { return "WavPlayback"; }

    /// @brief Borrow the configured WAV output path, before segment numbering.
    const std::string &path() const noexcept { return path_; }

    /// @brief Record to one file, truncating its existing contents at bind time.
    /// @throws Error If the format is unsupported for WAV output.
    explicit WavPlayback(std::string path, SampleFormat format);
    /// @brief Record numbered segments of the given audio duration. Zero selects a single file.
    /// @param write_mode Open mode for a single file.
    /// @note Numbered segments are created exclusively and existing numbers are skipped.
    /// @throws Error If the format is unsupported for WAV output or the duration is negative.
    WavPlayback(std::string path, SampleFormat format, std::chrono::seconds segment_duration,
                WavFile::WriteMode write_mode = WavFile::WriteMode::Truncate);
    ~WavPlayback() override { unbind(); }
    using ActiveBackend::bind;
    void bind(Capture &) = delete;

  private:
    Result<PortSpec> on_open(bool capture, PortSpec logical, std::chrono::microseconds period) override;
    RetCode on_bind_ready(PortSpec spec) noexcept override;
    RetCode on_start() noexcept override;
    void on_stop() noexcept override;
    void on_close() noexcept override;
    RetCode on_write(IBBlock input) noexcept override;
    RetCode open_file(PortSpec spec) noexcept;

    std::string path_;
    const SampleFormat format_;
    const std::chrono::seconds segment_duration_;
    const WavFile::WriteMode write_mode_;
    WavFile file_;
    std::string segment_path_;
    std::size_t segment_number_offset_ = 0;
    std::uint32_t segment_index_ = 0;
    std::size_t segment_frames_ = 0;
};

} // namespace aereborn

#endif

===== backend/wav_file.cpp =====
#include "wav_file.h"
#include "audio_types.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <cerrno>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <memory>

namespace aereborn
{
namespace
{

constexpr std::uint16_t pcm_tag = 1;
constexpr std::uint16_t float_tag = 3;

void append_u16(unsigned char *&out, std::uint16_t value) noexcept
{
    *out++ = static_cast<unsigned char>(value);
    *out++ = static_cast<unsigned char>(value >> 8);
}

void append_u32(unsigned char *&out, std::uint32_t value) noexcept
{
    append_u16(out, static_cast<std::uint16_t>(value));
    append_u16(out, static_cast<std::uint16_t>(value >> 16));
}

std::uint32_t wav_header_size(SampleFormat format) noexcept
{
    return format == SampleFormat::F32LE ? 58 : 44;
}

bool write_header(std::FILE *file, PortSpec spec, std::uint32_t data_size) noexcept
{
    const auto width = static_cast<std::uint16_t>(spec.channels * sample_bytes(spec.format));
    const auto rate = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(spec.bandwidth);
    const auto floating = spec.format == SampleFormat::F32LE;
    std::array<unsigned char, 58> header;
    auto *out = header.data();
    const auto append_tag = [&out](const char *tag) {
        std::memcpy(out, tag, 4);
        out += 4;
    };
    append_tag("RIFF");
    append_u32(out, wav_header_size(spec.format) - 8 + data_size + (data_size & 1));
    append_tag("WAVE");
    append_tag("fmt ");
    append_u32(out, floating ? 18 : 16);
    append_u16(out, floating ? float_tag : pcm_tag);
    append_u16(out, static_cast<std::uint16_t>(spec.channels));
    append_u32(out, rate);
    append_u32(out, rate * width);
    append_u16(out, width);
    append_u16(out, static_cast<std::uint16_t>(sample_bits(spec.format)));
    if (floating)
    {
        append_u16(out, 0);
        append_tag("fact");
        append_u32(out, 4);
        append_u32(out, data_size / width);
    }
    append_tag("data");
    append_u32(out, data_size);
    const auto size = static_cast<std::size_t>(out - header.data());
    return std::fwrite(header.data(), 1, size, file) == size && std::ferror(file) == 0;
}

std::FILE *open_file(const char *path, const char *mode) noexcept
{
#ifdef _MSC_VER
    std::FILE *file = nullptr;
    const auto result = ::fopen_s(&file, path, mode);
    if (result)
        errno = result;
    return file;
#else
    return std::fopen(path, mode);
#endif
}

bool seek_bytes(std::FILE *file, std::uint64_t position, int origin) noexcept
{
#if WINDOWS_OS_ENVIRONMENT
    return ::_fseeki64(file, static_cast<std::int64_t>(position), origin) == 0;
#else
    if (position > static_cast<std::uintmax_t>(std::numeric_limits<off_t>::max()))
        return false;
    return ::fseeko(file, static_cast<off_t>(position), origin) == 0;
#endif
}

std::int64_t tell_bytes(std::FILE *file) noexcept
{
#if WINDOWS_OS_ENVIRONMENT
    return ::_ftelli64(file);
#else
    return ::ftello(file);
#endif
}

struct WaveData
{
    PortSpec spec;
    std::uint64_t offset = 0;
    std::size_t size = 0;
    std::size_t frame_bytes = 0;
};

std::uint16_t read_u16(const unsigned char *p) noexcept
{
    return static_cast<std::uint16_t>(p[0] | (p[1] << 8));
}

std::uint32_t read_u32(const unsigned char *p) noexcept
{
    return read_u16(p) | (static_cast<std::uint32_t>(read_u16(p + 2)) << 16);
}

bool read_bytes(std::FILE *file, unsigned char *bytes, std::size_t size) noexcept
{
    return std::fread(bytes, 1, size, file) == size;
}

SampleFormat wav_format(std::uint16_t tag, std::uint16_t bits) noexcept
{
    if (tag == float_tag && (bits == 32 || bits == 64))
        return SampleFormat::F32LE;
    if (tag == pcm_tag)
    {
        switch (bits)
        {
        case 8: return SampleFormat::F32LE;
        case 16: return SampleFormat::S16LE;
        case 24: return SampleFormat::S24_3LE;
        case 32: return SampleFormat::S32LE;
        }
    }
    return {};
}

bool parse_format(std::FILE *file, std::uint32_t size, WaveData &data)
{
    std::array<unsigned char, 40> fmt{};
    const auto read_size = std::min<std::uint32_t>(size, fmt.size());
    if (read_size < 16 || !read_bytes(file, fmt.data(), read_size))
        return false;
    auto tag = read_u16(fmt.data());
    if (tag == 0xfffe) // WAVE_FORMAT_EXTENSIBLE
    {
        constexpr unsigned char subtype_suffix[] = {0, 0, 0, 0, 0x10, 0, 0x80, 0, 0, 0xaa, 0, 0x38, 0x9b, 0x71};
        const auto extension_size = read_u16(fmt.data() + 16);
        if (read_size < fmt.size() || extension_size < 22 || extension_size > size - 18 ||
            std::memcmp(fmt.data() + 26, subtype_suffix, sizeof(subtype_suffix)))
            return false;
        tag = read_u16(fmt.data() + 24);
    }
    const auto bits = read_u16(fmt.data() + 14);
    data.spec = {static_cast<BandWidth>(read_u32(fmt.data() + 4)), read_u16(fmt.data() + 2), wav_format(tag, bits)};
    data.frame_bytes = read_u16(fmt.data() + 12);
    return valid_pcm_spec(data.spec) && data.frame_bytes == data.spec.channels * (bits / 8);
}

RetCode parse_wav(std::FILE *file, WaveData &data)
{
    unsigned char header[12];
    if (!read_bytes(file, header, sizeof(header)) || std::memcmp(header, "RIFF", 4) ||
        std::memcmp(header + 8, "WAVE", 4))
        return RetCode::INVFMT;
    const auto limit = std::int64_t{8} + read_u32(header + 4);
    if (limit < 12 || !seek_bytes(file, 0, SEEK_END) || tell_bytes(file) < limit)
        return RetCode::IOFAILED;

    bool have_format = false;
    for (std::int64_t pos = 12; pos + 8 <= limit; )
    {
        unsigned char chunk[8];
        if (!seek_bytes(file, pos, SEEK_SET) || !read_bytes(file, chunk, sizeof(chunk)))
            return RetCode::IOFAILED;
        const auto size = read_u32(chunk + 4);
        const auto payload = pos + 8;
        pos = payload + size + (size & 1);
        if (pos > limit)
            return RetCode::IOFAILED;
        if (!have_format && !std::memcmp(chunk, "fmt ", 4))
        {
            if (!parse_format(file, size, data))
                return RetCode::INVFMT;
            have_format = true;
        }
        else if (!data.offset && !std::memcmp(chunk, "data", 4))
        {
            data.offset = payload;
            data.size = size;
        }
        if (have_format && data.offset)
        {
            if (data.size % data.frame_bytes)
                return RetCode::INVFMT;
            return seek_bytes(file, data.offset, SEEK_SET) ? RetCode::OK : RetCode::IOFAILED;
        }
    }
    return RetCode::INVFMT;
}

float read_f64(const unsigned char *bytes) noexcept
{
    static_assert(sizeof(double) == 8 && std::numeric_limits<double>::is_iec559, "WAV requires IEEE binary64");
    const auto bits = std::uint64_t{read_u32(bytes)} | (std::uint64_t{read_u32(bytes + 4)} << 32);
    double sample;
    std::memcpy(&sample, &bits, sizeof(sample));
    if (std::abs(sample) > std::numeric_limits<float>::max())
        return std::signbit(sample) ? -std::numeric_limits<float>::infinity() : std::numeric_limits<float>::infinity();
    return static_cast<float>(sample);
}

void convert_to_f32le(const unsigned char *src, std::size_t sample_bytes, std::size_t samples,
                      unsigned char *dst) noexcept
{
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < samples; ++i, src += sample_bytes)
    {
        const auto sample = sample_bytes == 1 ? (static_cast<int>(*src) - 128) / 128.0f : read_f64(src);
        std::uint32_t bits;
        std::memcpy(&bits, &sample, sizeof(bits));
        for (unsigned shift = 0; shift < 32; shift += 8)
            *dst++ = static_cast<unsigned char>(bits >> shift);
    }
}

} // namespace

WavFile::~WavFile()
{
    close();
}

RetCode WavFile::open_read(const std::string &path) noexcept
{
    if (file_ && writing_)
        return RetCode::BUSY;
    const auto close_input = [](std::FILE *file) noexcept { std::fclose(file); };
    std::unique_ptr<std::FILE, decltype(close_input)> file(open_file(path.c_str(), "rb"), close_input);
    if (!file)
        return RetCode::IOFAILED;
    WaveData data;
    const auto parsed = parse_wav(file.get(), data);
    if (!parsed)
        return parsed;
    const auto closed = close();
    if (!closed)
        return closed;

    file_ = file.release();
    spec_ = data.spec;
    data_offset_ = data.offset;
    file_frame_bytes_ = data.frame_bytes;
    frame_count_ = data.size / data.frame_bytes;
    return {};
}

bool WavFile::supports_write_format(SampleFormat format) noexcept
{
    return format == SampleFormat::S16LE || format == SampleFormat::S24_3LE ||
           format == SampleFormat::S32LE || format == SampleFormat::F32LE;
}

Result<std::size_t> WavFile::max_write_frames(PortSpec spec) noexcept
{
    const auto format = spec.format;
    if (!supports_write_format(format))
        return RetCode::INVFMT;
    if (!spec.valid())
        return RetCode::EPARAM;
    const auto sample_width = sample_bytes(format);
    const auto rate = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(spec.bandwidth);
    constexpr auto max_bytes = std::numeric_limits<std::uint32_t>::max();
    if (spec.channels > std::numeric_limits<std::uint16_t>::max() / sample_width ||
        spec.channels > max_bytes / sample_width / rate)
        return RetCode::TOOLARGE;

    // Leave room for the RIFF header and the pad byte of an odd data chunk.
    const auto limit = (max_bytes - (wav_header_size(format) - 8)) & ~std::uint32_t{1};
    return limit / (spec.channels * sample_width);
}

RetCode WavFile::open_write(const std::string &path, PortSpec spec, WriteMode mode) noexcept
{
    if (file_)
        return RetCode::BUSY;
    const auto limit = max_write_frames(spec);
    if (!limit)
        return limit.ret_code();
    const auto exclusive = mode == WriteMode::Exclusive;
    auto *file = open_file(path.c_str(), exclusive ? "wbx" : "wb");
    if (!file)
        return exclusive && errno == EEXIST ? RetCode::BUSY : RetCode::IOFAILED;

    if (!write_header(file, spec, 0) || std::fflush(file) != 0)
    {
        std::fclose(file);
        return RetCode::IOFAILED;
    }

    file_ = file;
    writing_ = true;
    spec_ = spec;
    data_offset_ = wav_header_size(spec.format);
    file_frame_bytes_ = spec.channels * sample_bytes(spec.format);
    write_limit_ = limit.value();
    return {};
}

RetCode WavFile::close() noexcept
{
    if (!file_)
        return {};
    const auto prior_ok = std::ferror(file_) == 0;
    bool finalized = true;
    if (writing_ && frame_count_)
    {
        const auto size = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(frame_count_ * file_frame_bytes_);
        std::clearerr(file_);
        if (size & 1)
            finalized = seek_bytes(file_, data_offset_ + size, SEEK_SET) && std::fputc(0, file_) != EOF;
        finalized = finalized && seek_bytes(file_, 0, SEEK_SET) && write_header(file_, spec_, size);
    }
    const auto closed = std::fclose(file_);
    file_ = nullptr;
    writing_ = false;
    spec_ = {};
    frame_count_ = position_ = 0;
    return prior_ok && finalized && closed == 0 ? RetCode::OK : RetCode::IOFAILED;
}

RetCode WavFile::flush() noexcept
{
    if (!writing_)
        return RetCode::ESTATE;
    return std::fflush(file_) != 0 || std::ferror(file_) ? RetCode::IOFAILED : RetCode::OK;
}

RetCode WavFile::status() const noexcept
{
    if (!file_)
        return RetCode::ESTATE;
    return std::ferror(file_) ? RetCode::IOFAILED : RetCode::OK;
}

RetCode WavFile::seek(std::size_t frame) noexcept
{
    if (!file_)
        return RetCode::ESTATE;
    if (frame > frame_count_)
        return RetCode::EPARAM;
    // Allow readers to retry while preserving any write error for close().
    if (!writing_)
        std::clearerr(file_);
    if (!seek_bytes(file_, data_offset_ + frame * file_frame_bytes_, SEEK_SET))
        return RetCode::IOFAILED;
    position_ = frame;
    return {};
}

Result<std::size_t> WavFile::read(OBBlock output) noexcept
{
    if (!file_ || writing_)
        return RetCode::ESTATE;
    if (output.spec() != spec_)
        return RetCode::INVFMT;
    const auto frames = std::min(output.frames(), frame_count_ - position_);
    if (!frames)
        return frames;

    auto *dst = output.byte_view().data();
    if (file_frame_bytes_ == spec_.channels * sample_bytes(spec_.format))
    {
        if (!read_bytes(file_, dst, frames * file_frame_bytes_))
            return RetCode::IOFAILED;
    }
    else
    {
        // Convert extra on-disk formats in bounded groups of interleaved samples.
        std::array<unsigned char, 4096> scratch;
        const auto sample_width = file_frame_bytes_ / spec_.channels;
        auto samples = frames * spec_.channels;
        while (samples)
        {
            const auto count = std::min(samples, scratch.size() / sample_width);
            if (!read_bytes(file_, scratch.data(), count * sample_width))
                return RetCode::IOFAILED;
            convert_to_f32le(scratch.data(), sample_width, count, dst);
            samples -= count;
            dst += count * sizeof(float);
        }
    }
    position_ += frames;
    return frames;
}

RetCode WavFile::write(IBBlock input) noexcept
{
    if (!writing_)
        return RetCode::ESTATE;
    if (input.spec() != spec_)
        return RetCode::INVFMT;
    if (std::ferror(file_))
        return RetCode::IOFAILED;
    if (input.frames() > write_limit_ - position_)
        return RetCode::TOOLARGE;
    if (!input.frames())
        return {};

    if (std::fwrite(input.byte_view().data(), file_frame_bytes_, input.frames(), file_) != input.frames() ||
        std::ferror(file_))
        return RetCode::IOFAILED;
    position_ += input.frames();
    frame_count_ = std::max(frame_count_, position_);
    return {};
}

} // namespace aereborn

===== backend/wav_file.h =====
#ifndef AEREBORN_BACKEND_WAV_FILE_H
#define AEREBORN_BACKEND_WAV_FILE_H

#include "core/types.h"
#include <cstdio>
#include <string>

namespace aereborn
{

/// @brief Read or write one RIFF/WAVE file using frame offsets.
/// @note Serialize access. Unsigned 8-bit PCM and 64-bit float are read as F32LE.
class WavFile
{
  public:
    enum class WriteMode { Truncate, Exclusive };

    WavFile() = default;
    ~WavFile();
    WavFile(const WavFile &) = delete;
    WavFile &operator=(const WavFile &) = delete;

    /// @brief Open a WAV file at frame zero. Invalid input leaves the current reader usable.
    RetCode open_read(const std::string &path) noexcept;
    /// @brief Open a file for writing. Exclusive returns RetCode::BUSY for an existing path.
    /// @pre Close the current file before opening an output.
    RetCode open_write(const std::string &path, PortSpec spec, WriteMode mode) noexcept;
    /// @brief Close the file and finalize the header when writing.
    RetCode close() noexcept;
    /// @brief Flush buffered output to the operating system and report any write error.
    RetCode flush() noexcept;
    /// @brief Report an I/O error or RetCode::ESTATE if there is no open file.
    RetCode status() const noexcept;
    /// @brief Report whether the sample format is supported for RIFF/WAVE output.
    static bool supports_write_format(SampleFormat format) noexcept;
    /// @brief Return the RIFF frame limit for a supported output specification.
    static Result<std::size_t> max_write_frames(PortSpec spec) noexcept;

    /// @brief Return the interleaved PCM specification used for I/O.
    PortSpec spec() const noexcept { return spec_; }
    /// @brief Return the number of frames in the open file.
    std::size_t frame_count() const noexcept { return frame_count_; }
    /// @brief Return the zero-based index of the next frame to read or write.
    std::size_t tell() const noexcept { return position_; }
    /// @brief Seek to an existing frame or to the end of the data.
    RetCode seek(std::size_t frame) noexcept;
    /// @brief Read up to output.frames() frames, returning zero at EOF.
    Result<std::size_t> read(OBBlock output) noexcept;
    /// @brief Write an interleaved block at the current frame position.
    RetCode write(IBBlock input) noexcept;

  private:
    std::FILE *file_ = nullptr;
    bool writing_ = false;
    PortSpec spec_;
    std::uint64_t data_offset_ = 0;
    std::size_t file_frame_bytes_ = 0;
    std::size_t frame_count_ = 0;
    std::size_t position_ = 0;
    std::size_t write_limit_ = 0;
};

} // namespace aereborn

#endif

===== include/audio_interface.h =====
#ifndef AEREBORN_AUDIO_INTERFACE_H
#define AEREBORN_AUDIO_INTERFACE_H

#include "audio_types.h"
#include <chrono>
#include <cstddef>
#include <initializer_list>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace aereborn
{

class CommandQueue;
class GraphService;
class DeviceService;

/// @brief Hardware endpoint options. RECOVERSOFT and RECOVERHARD are mutually exclusive.
enum class DevFlags : std::uint32_t
{
    NONE        = 0,       ///< No options enabled.
    RECOVERSOFT = 1u << 0, ///< Wait for unavailable devices and retry I/O failures. Keep running on timeouts.
    RECOVERHARD = 1u << 1, ///< Also recover on timeouts and reopen periodically.
    MEMCPY      = 1u << 2  ///< Force ALSA RW_INTERLEAVED. Accepted and ignored on Windows.
};

constexpr DevFlags operator|(DevFlags lhs, DevFlags rhs) noexcept
{
    return static_cast<DevFlags>(static_cast<std::uint32_t>(lhs) | static_cast<std::uint32_t>(rhs));
}

constexpr DevFlags operator&(DevFlags lhs, DevFlags rhs) noexcept
{
    return static_cast<DevFlags>(static_cast<std::uint32_t>(lhs) & static_cast<std::uint32_t>(rhs));
}

/// @brief Test whether every bit in flag is set.
constexpr bool has_flag(DevFlags flags, DevFlags flag) noexcept
{
    return (flags & flag) == flag;
}

/// @brief Graph-side configuration for a hardware audio endpoint.
/// @note Empty name or "default" selects the system default. ALSA passes names containing ':' through
///       and expands a bare card name to hw:CARD=<name>,DEV=<device>. WASAPI accepts an endpoint ID
///       or a UTF-8 friendly name and ignores device.
/// @note The backend negotiates sample format and rate. The opened stream must accept exactly channels.
///       ALSA hw: applies that constraint to the hardware. ALSA PCM plugins can convert below the stream.
struct DevConfig
{
    std::string name;
    unsigned int device = 0;
    BandWidth bandwidth = BandWidth::Full;
    BlockPeriod period = BlockPeriod::INR_10MS; ///< Device batch and queue budget in milliseconds.
    std::size_t channels = 2;
};

/// @brief Build a weighted channel map for AudioCenter::create_matrix().
/// @note Indices are zero-based and follow the direction from input to output.
///       Construction and editing keep the first error. Later edits leave the map alone.
class ChannelMap
{
  public:
    /// @brief Construct an empty map with status EPARAM.
    ChannelMap() noexcept = default;
    /// @brief Construct a silent map with the given input and output channel counts.
    explicit ChannelMap(std::size_t input_channels, std::size_t output_channels) noexcept;

    /// @brief Route every input to the corresponding output at unity gain.
    static ChannelMap identity(std::size_t channels) noexcept;
    /// @brief Pick inputs in output order. An input may appear more than once.
    static ChannelMap select(std::size_t input_channels, const std::vector<std::size_t> &sources) noexcept;
    /// @copydoc select(std::size_t, const std::vector<std::size_t>&)
    static ChannelMap select(std::size_t input_channels, std::initializer_list<std::size_t> sources) noexcept;
    /// @brief Pick count consecutive input channels starting at first.
    static ChannelMap slice(std::size_t input_channels, std::size_t first, std::size_t count) noexcept;
    /// @brief Average the input channels into a single output.
    static ChannelMap to_mono(std::size_t input_channels) noexcept;
    /// @brief Copy one input channel to every output.
    static ChannelMap from_mono(std::size_t output_channels) noexcept;
    /// @brief Copy row-major coefficients, one row of input weights per output.
    static ChannelMap dense(std::size_t input_channels, std::size_t output_channels,
                            const std::vector<float> &coefficients) noexcept;
    /// @copydoc dense(std::size_t, std::size_t, const std::vector<float>&)
    static ChannelMap dense(std::size_t input_channels, std::size_t output_channels,
                            std::initializer_list<float> coefficients) noexcept;

    /// @brief Set the weight from one input to one output. Zero disconnects that route.
    ChannelMap &route(std::size_t from, std::size_t to, float weight = 1.0f) noexcept;
    /// @brief Replace an output's weights with gain divided equally among distinct inputs.
    ChannelMap &mix(const std::vector<std::size_t> &from, std::size_t to, float gain = 1.0f) noexcept;
    /// @copydoc mix(const std::vector<std::size_t>&, std::size_t, float)
    ChannelMap &mix(std::initializer_list<std::size_t> from, std::size_t to, float gain = 1.0f) noexcept;
    /// @brief Multiply the weights feeding one output by factor.
    ChannelMap &scale_output(std::size_t to, float factor) noexcept;

    /// @brief Report whether this map can be passed to AudioCenter::create_matrix().
    bool valid() const noexcept;
    /// @brief Report EPARAM for invalid maps or edits and TOOLARGE for capacity or allocation failures.
    RetCode status() const noexcept;
    std::size_t input_channels() const noexcept { return input_channels_; }
    std::size_t output_channels() const noexcept { return output_channels_; }
    /// @brief Return a routing weight, or zero for an invalid map or index.
    float weight(std::size_t from, std::size_t to) const noexcept;
    /// @brief Borrow row-major coefficients until the map is edited, moved, or destroyed.
    const std::vector<float> &coefficients() const noexcept { return coefficients_; }

  private:
    static ChannelMap select(std::size_t input_channels, const std::size_t *sources, std::size_t count) noexcept;
    static ChannelMap dense(std::size_t input_channels, std::size_t output_channels,
                            const float *coefficients, std::size_t count) noexcept;
    ChannelMap &mix(const std::size_t *from, std::size_t count, std::size_t to, float gain) noexcept;
    float *output_row(std::size_t to) noexcept;

    std::size_t input_channels_ = 0;
    std::size_t output_channels_ = 0;
    std::vector<float> coefficients_;
    RetCode status_{RetCode::EPARAM};
};

/// @brief Control a graph of hardware, WAV, callback, and push/pull endpoints, mixers, and channel matrices.
/// @note Control calls serialize on a control thread and may block.
/// @pre All external calls must return before destruction begins.
class AudioCenter
{
  public:
    /// @brief Construct a graph with the given software clock period.
    /// @throws std::runtime_error If the period is unsupported or the control thread cannot start.
    explicit AudioCenter(BlockPeriod period = BlockPeriod::INR_10MS);

    /// @brief Unbind all endpoints, finalize recordings, and stop the software clock.
    ~AudioCenter();

    AudioCenter(const AudioCenter &) = delete;
    AudioCenter &operator=(const AudioCenter &) = delete;

    /// @brief Create a hardware capture endpoint with an F32P graph output.
    /// @param config Device selection and graph sample rate, channel count, and device I/O budget.
    /// @param flags Optional recovery and access mode flags.
    /// @return A node handle, or an empty handle on failure. See last_error().
    /// @note With recovery enabled, a device that fails with DEVLOST or IOFAILED can start offline.
    ///       The endpoint supplies silence while offline and retries while in the running plan.
    NodeId create_idev(const DevConfig &config = {}, DevFlags flags = DevFlags::NONE);

    /// @brief Create a hardware playback endpoint with an F32P graph input.
    /// @param config Device selection and graph sample rate, channel count, and device I/O budget.
    /// @param flags Optional recovery and access mode flags.
    /// @return A node handle, or an empty handle on failure. See last_error().
    /// @note With recovery enabled, a device that fails with DEVLOST or IOFAILED can start offline.
    ///       The endpoint discards input while offline and retries while in the running plan.
    NodeId create_odev(const DevConfig &config = {}, DevFlags flags = DevFlags::NONE);

    /// @brief Probe an existing WAV file and create a source for its sample rate and channels.
    /// @param repeat Zero plays once, a positive value adds passes, and a negative value loops.
    /// @param io_period External I/O batch duration used for queue sizing.
    /// @return A node handle, or an empty handle on failure. See last_error().
    /// @note Restarting resumes at the next frame to read. Recreate the node to rewind.
    NodeId create_iwav(const std::string &path, int repeat = 0,
                       std::chrono::microseconds io_period = std::chrono::milliseconds(10));

    /// @brief Create a WAV output and open its file without overwriting an existing file.
    /// @param spec WAV sample rate, channels, and one of S16LE, S24_3LE, S32LE, or F32LE.
    /// @param segment_duration Positive values select numbered files of this audio duration.
    /// @param io_period External I/O batch duration used for queue sizing.
    /// @return A node handle, or an empty handle on failure. An existing single file reports BUSY.
    /// @note Stop drains buffered audio. Removal and destruction finalize the open file.
    NodeId create_owav(const std::string &path, PortSpec spec = {BandWidth::Full, 2, SampleFormat::S16LE},
                       std::chrono::seconds segment_duration = std::chrono::seconds(0),
                       std::chrono::microseconds io_period = std::chrono::milliseconds(10));

    /// @brief Create a callback source with an F32P graph output at spec's sample rate and channel count.
    /// @param spec Fixed interleaved PCM specification (S16LE, S24_3LE, S24LE, S32LE, or F32LE).
    /// @param callback Non-null function that fills a zero-initialized output buffer on a worker thread.
    /// @param user_data Caller pointer that must stay valid while the endpoint can call the callback.
    /// @param io_period Duration used to size batches and the queue. The batch frame count rounds down.
    /// @return A node handle, or an empty handle on failure. See last_error().
    NodeId create_icallback(PortSpec spec, AudioCallback callback, void *user_data = nullptr,
                            std::chrono::microseconds io_period = std::chrono::milliseconds(10));

    /// @brief Create a callback sink with an F32P graph input at spec's sample rate and channel count.
    /// @param spec Fixed interleaved PCM specification (S16LE, S24_3LE, S24LE, S32LE, or F32LE).
    /// @param callback Non-null function that receives read-only input on a worker thread.
    /// @param user_data Caller pointer that must stay valid while the endpoint can call the callback.
    /// @param io_period Duration used to size batches and the queue. The batch frame count rounds down.
    /// @return A node handle, or an empty handle on failure. See last_error().
    /// @note Stop and committed pause drain buffered audio before returning. The last batch may be shorter.
    NodeId create_ocallback(PortSpec spec, AudioCallback callback, void *user_data = nullptr,
                            std::chrono::microseconds io_period = std::chrono::milliseconds(10));

    /// @brief Create a caller-driven PCM source with an F32P graph output.
    /// @param spec Fixed sample rate, channel count, and interleaved PCM format (S16LE, S24_3LE, S24LE, S32LE, or F32LE).
    /// @param io_period Burst duration budget used to size the queue.
    /// @return A node handle, or an empty handle on failure. See last_error().
    NodeId create_ipush(PortSpec spec, std::chrono::microseconds io_period = std::chrono::milliseconds(10));

    /// @brief Create a caller-driven PCM sink with an F32P graph input.
    /// @param spec Fixed sample rate, channel count, and one of create_ipush()'s interleaved PCM formats.
    /// @param io_period Burst duration budget used to size the queue.
    /// @return A node handle, or an empty handle on failure. See last_error().
    /// @note Stop and committed pause discard buffered frames.
    NodeId create_opull(PortSpec spec, std::chrono::microseconds io_period = std::chrono::milliseconds(10));

    /// @brief Offer interleaved PCM to a push source without waiting for queue space.
    /// @param node Handle from create_ipush(), with port index zero.
    /// @param data Borrowed PCM in the declared format, valid for this call. May be null for zero frames.
    /// @param frames Number of input frames to offer.
    /// @return Frames accepted. Resume a short write at this frame offset.
    /// @note Intended for application threads. Calls serialize per node with each other and lifecycle changes.
    ///       Handle lookup and transfer run on the calling thread.
    /// @note last_error() reports OK on success or backpressure, ESTATE when stopped, or EPORT, ENODE, ETYPE,
    ///       EPARAM, or TOOLARGE for invalid handles, buffers, or sizes.
    std::size_t push(NodeId node, const void *data, std::size_t frames) noexcept;

    /// @brief Read available interleaved PCM from a pull sink without waiting for audio.
    /// @param node Handle from create_opull(), with port index zero.
    /// @param data Borrowed writable PCM in the declared format, valid for this call. May be null for zero frames.
    /// @param frames Maximum number of output frames to read.
    /// @return Frames written. The unwritten suffix is left intact.
    /// @note Uses the same serialization and last_error() rules as push().
    std::size_t pull(NodeId node, void *data, std::size_t frames) noexcept;

    /// @brief Create an F32P mixer with 1 to 64 inputs. Returns an empty handle on failure.
    NodeId create_mixer(BandWidth bandwidth, std::size_t channels, std::size_t inputs);
    /// @brief Create a node by copying a channel map and applying the given linear gain.
    /// @return A node handle, or an empty handle on failure.
    /// @note Use ChannelMap::identity(channels) for a gain-only node. Keep the map available for reading until this call returns. See last_error() on failure.
    NodeId create_matrix(BandWidth bandwidth, const ChannelMap &map, float gain = 1.0f);

    /// @brief Create a sample rate and format converter. Input and output must have the same channel count.
    /// @return A node handle, or an empty handle on failure. See last_error().
    /// @note Use create_matrix() to change the channel count.
    NodeId create_convert(PortSpec input, PortSpec output);

    /// @brief Read the graph-side specification of an output port.
    /// @return The port specification, or an invalid specification on failure. See last_error().
    PortSpec output_spec(NodeId port) const;

    /// @brief Stage a connection between ports with identical sample rate, channel count and sample format.
    /// @return INVFMT if the port specifications differ.
    RetCode connect(NodeId from, NodeId to);
    /// @brief Stage removal of the given connection.
    RetCode disconnect(NodeId from, NodeId to);
    /// @brief Stage removal of the connection at the destination input.
    /// @note After commit, unpaused outputs without a source receive silence while the graph runs.
    RetCode disconnect(NodeId to);
    /// @brief Remove a node and its connections from the draft. The handle must select port zero.
    /// @note Endpoint removal unbinds immediately, including when an old plan still contains it.
    RetCode remove(NodeId node);

    /// @brief Publish the draft and reconcile backend starts and stops while running.
    /// @note Graph preparation failure retains the current plan. If a backend fails to start
    /// after publication, the new plan remains current and the graph stops. Hardware endpoints
    /// with recovery enabled handle their start failures locally.
    RetCode commit();
    /// @brief Commit pending edits, start the backends in the plan, and start the clock.
    RetCode start();
    /// @brief Stop the backends and clock. WAV and callback outputs drain and pull sinks discard queued audio.
    /// @note WAV files stay open.
    RetCode stop() noexcept;

    /// @brief Stage a node pause or resume for the next commit. The handle must select port zero.
    RetCode set_paused(NodeId node, bool paused);
    /// @brief Set a matrix's linear gain target. The handle must select port zero.
    RetCode set_gain(NodeId matrix, float gain);
    /// @brief Set a mixer's mute flag for the given input port.
    RetCode set_muted(NodeId mixer_port, bool muted);
    /// @brief Set a mixer's high-priority flag for the given input port.
    RetCode set_high_priority(NodeId mixer_port, bool high);

    /// @brief Describe the configured graph as JSON with live backend metrics and committed execution order.
    /// @return The snapshot, or an empty string on failure. See last_error().
    std::string dump_json() const;

    /// @brief Return the status of the most recent control, diagnostic, or push/pull call on this thread.
    /// @note Successful calls store OK. Construction, destruction, and this query leave it alone.
    static RetCode last_error() noexcept;

  private:
    std::unique_ptr<CommandQueue> commands_;
    std::unique_ptr<GraphService> graph_;
    std::unique_ptr<DeviceService> devices_;
};

} // namespace aereborn

#endif // AEREBORN_AUDIO_INTERFACE_H

===== include/audio_types.h =====
#ifndef AEREBORN_AUDIO_TYPES_H
#define AEREBORN_AUDIO_TYPES_H

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>

#if defined(_WIN32) || defined(_WIN64)
#include <sdkddkver.h>
#if _WIN32_WINNT >= 0x0600 // Windows Vista
#define WINDOWS_OS_ENVIRONMENT 1
#else
#define WINDOWS_OS_ENVIRONMENT 0
#endif
#else
#define WINDOWS_OS_ENVIRONMENT 0
#endif

#if defined(__linux__) || defined(__linux) || defined(linux)
#define LINUX_OS_ENVIRONMENT 1
#else
#define LINUX_OS_ENVIRONMENT 0
#endif

namespace aereborn
{

/// @brief Non-throwing status code shared across engine boundaries and public APIs.
/// @note Stores a single 32-bit code without allocating.
class RetCode
{
  public:
    /// @brief Status values shared with backend workers and control callers.
    /// @note BUSY and IOFAILED avoid collisions with platform errno macros.
    enum Code : std::int32_t
    {
        FAILED = -18, ///< Generic unclassified failure
        EPARAM,       ///< Invalid argument
        EPORT,        ///< Port index out of range or nonzero port passed to node-level call
        ETYPE,        ///< Node has a different type
        ESTATE,       ///< Object is not prepared, committed, or in a valid state
        BUSY,         ///< Resource busy or control call from engine clock thread
        INPUTBUSY,    ///< Destination input port already has a source
        ENODE,        ///< Node does not belong to this engine or was removed
        ECONN,        ///< Connection does not exist
        IOFAILED,     ///< Device open, file, or underlying I/O failure
        INVFMT,       ///< Formats or port layouts do not match
        TOOLARGE,     ///< Requested capacity, frame count, or port count exceeds limit
        DUPLICATE,    ///< These ports are already connected
        CYCLE,        ///< Connection would create a feedback cycle
        DEVLOST,      ///< Audio device is no longer available
        EOS,          ///< Finite stream reached end of stream
        CANCELLED,    ///< Blocking I/O operation was cancelled
        TIMEOUT,      ///< Async I/O operation timed out

        // Non-error status
        OK = 0,       ///< Success
        NOACTION = 1  ///< Idempotent operation performed no action
    };

    constexpr RetCode(Code code = OK) noexcept : code_(code) {}

    /// @brief Check whether the operation returned OK.
    constexpr explicit operator bool() const noexcept { return code_ == OK; }
    constexpr Code code() const noexcept { return code_; }

    friend constexpr bool operator==(RetCode lhs, RetCode rhs) noexcept { return lhs.code_ == rhs.code_; }
    friend constexpr bool operator!=(RetCode lhs, RetCode rhs) noexcept { return lhs.code_ != rhs.code_; }

    constexpr const char *what() const noexcept
    {
        switch (code_)
        {
        case OK:         return "Success";
        case NOACTION:   return "No action";
        case FAILED:     return "Operation failed";
        case EPARAM:     return "Invalid argument";
        case EPORT:      return "Port index is out of range";
        case ETYPE:      return "Node has a different type";
        case ESTATE:     return "Object is not prepared, committed, or bound";
        case BUSY:       return "The current execution context or state prevents this operation";
        case INPUTBUSY:  return "Input already has a source (use a mixer for multiple sources)";
        case ENODE:      return "Node does not belong to this engine or was removed";
        case ECONN:      return "Connection does not exist";
        case IOFAILED:   return "I/O operation failed";
        case INVFMT:     return "Formats or port layouts do not match";
        case TOOLARGE:   return "Requested capacity exceeds the supported size";
        case DUPLICATE:  return "These ports are already connected";
        case CYCLE:      return "Connection would create a feedback cycle";
        case DEVLOST:    return "Audio device is no longer available";
        case EOS:        return "End of stream";
        case CANCELLED:  return "I/O operation was cancelled";
        case TIMEOUT:    return "I/O operation timed out";
        }
        return "Unknown error code";
    }

  private:
    Code code_;
};

static_assert(sizeof(RetCode) == 4, "RetCode must remain a 4-byte value");

/// @brief Supported audio bandwidths (sample rates in Hz).
enum class BandWidth : std::uint32_t
{
    Unknown       = 0,
    Narrow        = 8000,
    QuarterCD     = 11025,
    Wide          = 16000,
    HalfCD        = 22050,
    SemiSuperWide = 24000,
    SuperWideband = 32000,
    CDQuality     = 44100,
    Full          = 48000,
    DoubleCD      = 88200,
    UltraFull     = 96000
};

/// @brief Supported durations of one engine tick, in milliseconds.
enum class BlockPeriod : std::uint32_t
{
    INR_02MS = 2,
    INR_05MS = 5,
    INR_10MS = 10,
    INR_20MS = 20,
    INR_40MS = 40
};

/// @brief Sample storage format, including channel layout and byte order.
/// @note F32P stores native planar float32. All other formats store interleaved little-endian PCM.
enum class SampleFormat : std::uint32_t
{
    F32P = 0, ///< Native planar float32
    S16LE,    ///< Signed 16-bit interleaved little-endian PCM
    S24_3LE,  ///< Signed 24-bit PCM packed into 3 bytes per sample
    S24LE,    ///< Signed 24-bit PCM stored in a 4-byte slot per sample
    S32LE,    ///< Signed 32-bit interleaved little-endian PCM
    F32LE     ///< 32-bit IEEE float interleaved little-endian PCM
};

/// @brief Fixed bandwidth, channel count, and format declared by a port or PCM boundary.
/// @note Connections require all three fields to match. Each frame contains one sample per channel.
struct PortSpec
{
    /// @brief Construct an empty placeholder with no bandwidth or channels.
    constexpr PortSpec() noexcept = default;

    /// @brief Specify the bandwidth in Hz, channels per frame and sample format.
    constexpr PortSpec(BandWidth bandwidth, std::size_t channels, SampleFormat format) noexcept
        : channels(channels), bandwidth(bandwidth), format(format)
    {
    }

    constexpr bool operator==(const PortSpec &other) const noexcept
    {
        return bandwidth == other.bandwidth && channels == other.channels && format == other.format;
    }
    constexpr bool operator!=(const PortSpec &other) const noexcept { return !(*this == other); }

    /// @brief Check whether bandwidth, channels, and format are valid.
    bool valid() const noexcept;

    /// @brief Return the maximum frame count in one block for buffer sizing.
    std::size_t max_frames(BlockPeriod period) const noexcept
    {
        const auto units = std::uint64_t{static_cast<std::uint32_t>(bandwidth)} * static_cast<std::uint32_t>(period);
        return static_cast<std::size_t>((units + 999) / 1000);
    }

    /// @brief Return the actual frame count for a tick.
    std::size_t frames(BlockPeriod period, std::uint64_t tick) const noexcept
    {
        const auto units = std::uint64_t{static_cast<std::uint32_t>(bandwidth)} * static_cast<std::uint32_t>(period);
        const auto fraction = units % 1000;
        if (fraction == 0)
            return static_cast<std::size_t>(units / 1000);
        const auto start = (tick % 1000) * fraction;
        return static_cast<std::size_t>(units / 1000 + (start + fraction + 999) / 1000 - (start + 999) / 1000);
    }

    /// @brief Return the zero-based frame position at the start of a tick (fractional positions round up).
    std::uint64_t frame_position(BlockPeriod period, std::uint64_t tick) const noexcept
    {
        const auto units = std::uint64_t{static_cast<std::uint32_t>(bandwidth)} * static_cast<std::uint32_t>(period);
        // Split whole seconds from the remainder to avoid multiplying tick by the sample rate.
        return (tick / 1000) * units + ((tick % 1000) * units + 999) / 1000;
    }

    std::size_t channels = 0;
    BandWidth bandwidth = BandWidth::Unknown;
    SampleFormat format{};
};

/// @brief Exchange interleaved PCM with an AudioCenter callback endpoint.
/// @param input Read-only PCM from the graph. Null for a source callback.
/// @param output Writable PCM feeding the graph. Null for a sink callback.
/// @param frames Number of frames in the batch. A sink's final drain batch may be shorter.
/// @param spec Fixed PCM specification declared at creation.
/// @param user_data Opaque caller pointer supplied at creation.
/// @note Each endpoint calls its callback on a dedicated backend worker thread.
/// @note Buffers are borrowed for this call. Callbacks must return without throwing or calling AudioCenter control methods.
using AudioCallback = void (*)(const void *input, void *output, std::size_t frames, PortSpec spec, void *user_data);

/// @brief Unified 64-bit node and port handle.
/// @note High 48 bits identify the node (0 = empty/invalid). Low 16 bits select the zero-based port
///       (default 0; 0xFFFF is reserved as an out-of-range sentinel to prevent wraparound).
struct NodeId
{
    static constexpr std::uint64_t port_mask = 0xFFFFu;
    static constexpr std::uint64_t max_node = 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFull;
    static constexpr std::size_t invalid_port = static_cast<std::size_t>(port_mask);

    std::uint64_t raw = 0;

    /// @brief Construct a handle from a 48-bit node identity and an optional port index.
    /// @return An empty handle if the node identity is zero or exceeds 48 bits.
    static constexpr NodeId make(std::uint64_t node_id, std::size_t port_idx = 0) noexcept
    {
        if (node_id > max_node)
            return {};
        return NodeId{node_id << 16}[port_idx];
    }

    constexpr explicit operator bool() const noexcept { return node() != 0; }
    constexpr std::uint64_t node() const noexcept { return raw >> 16; }
    constexpr std::size_t port() const noexcept { return static_cast<std::size_t>(raw & port_mask); }
    constexpr NodeId base() const noexcept { return {raw & ~port_mask}; }

    /// @brief Select a specific port on this node (e.g. mix[0], mix[1]).
    constexpr NodeId operator[](std::size_t port_idx) const noexcept
    {
        if (!*this)
            return {};
        const auto port = port_idx < invalid_port ? static_cast<std::uint64_t>(port_idx) : port_mask;
        return {base().raw | port};
    }

    constexpr bool operator==(NodeId other) const noexcept { return raw == other.raw; }
    constexpr bool operator!=(NodeId other) const noexcept { return raw != other.raw; }
    constexpr bool operator<(NodeId other) const noexcept { return raw < other.raw; }
};

static_assert(sizeof(NodeId) == 8, "NodeId must remain an 8-byte value");

} // namespace aereborn

#endif // AEREBORN_AUDIO_TYPES_H

===== diag/inspect.cpp =====
#include "inspect.h"

#include "backend/wasapi_backend.h"
#include "backend/wav_backend.h"

namespace
