const char *state_name(Engine::State state)
{
    switch (state)
    {
    case Engine::State::Unprepared: return "Unprepared";
    case Engine::State::Ready: return "Ready";
    case Engine::State::Running: return "Running";
    }
    return "?";
}

const char *state_name(ActiveBackend::State state)
{
    switch (state)
    {
    case ActiveBackend::State::Stopped: return "Stopped";
    case ActiveBackend::State::Running: return "Running";
    case ActiveBackend::State::Draining: return "Draining";
    case ActiveBackend::State::Stopping: return "Stopping";
    }
    return "?";
}

const char *format_name(SampleFormat format)
{
    switch (format)
    {
    case SampleFormat::F32P: return "F32P";
    case SampleFormat::S16LE: return "S16LE";
    case SampleFormat::S24_3LE: return "S24_3LE";
    case SampleFormat::S24LE: return "S24LE";
    case SampleFormat::S32LE: return "S32LE";
    case SampleFormat::F32LE: return "F32LE";
    }
    return "?";
}

const char *error_code_name(RetCode::Code code)
{
    switch (code)
    {
    case RetCode::OK: return "OK";
    case RetCode::NOACTION: return "NOACTION";
    case RetCode::FAILED: return "FAILED";
    case RetCode::EPARAM: return "EPARAM";
    case RetCode::EPORT: return "EPORT";
    case RetCode::ETYPE: return "ETYPE";
    case RetCode::TOOLARGE: return "TOOLARGE";
    case RetCode::ESTATE: return "ESTATE";
    case RetCode::BUSY: return "BUSY";
    case RetCode::ENODE: return "ENODE";
    case RetCode::ECONN: return "ECONN";
    case RetCode::DUPLICATE: return "DUPLICATE";
    case RetCode::INPUTBUSY: return "INPUTBUSY";
    case RetCode::CYCLE: return "CYCLE";
    case RetCode::INVFMT: return "INVFMT";
    case RetCode::IOFAILED: return "IOFAILED";
    case RetCode::DEVLOST: return "DEVLOST";
    case RetCode::EOS: return "EOS";
    case RetCode::CANCELLED: return "CANCELLED";
    case RetCode::TIMEOUT: return "TIMEOUT";
    }
    return "Unknown";
}

void write_json_string(std::ostream &out, const std::string &text)
{
    const char hex[] = "0123456789abcdef";
    out << '"';
    for (const unsigned char ch : text)
    {
        if (ch == '"' || ch == '\\')
            out << '\\' << static_cast<char>(ch);
        else if (ch < 0x20)
            out << "\\u00" << hex[ch >> 4] << hex[ch & 0x0f];
        else
            out << static_cast<char>(ch);
    }
    out << '"';
}

void write_json_spec(std::ostream &out, PortSpec spec)
{
    out << "{\"rate\":" << static_cast<std::uint32_t>(spec.bandwidth)
        << ",\"channels\":" << spec.channels << ",\"format\":";
    write_json_string(out, format_name(spec.format));
    out << '}';
}

void write_json_ports(std::ostream &out, View<const PortSpec> ports)
{
    out << '[';
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < ports.size(); ++i)
    {
        if (i)
            out << ',';
        write_json_spec(out, ports[i]);
    }
    out << ']';
}

template <class Endpoint>
void write_json_queue(std::ostream &out, const Endpoint &endpoint, const char *loss, std::size_t lost)
{
    const auto online = endpoint.online();
    const auto capacity = endpoint.capacity();
    std::size_t frames = 0;
    out << ",\"queue\":{\"bound\":" << endpoint.bound() << ",\"online\":" << online
        << ",\"steady\":" << (online && endpoint.steady()) << ",\"capacity\":" << capacity << ",\"frames\":";
    if (capacity && endpoint.estimate_queued_frames(frames))
        out << frames;
    else
        out << "null";
    out << ",\"" << loss << "\":" << lost << '}';
}

void write_json_node(std::ostream &out, const ConfiguredNode &entry, bool in_plan)
{
    const auto &node = *entry.node;
    out << "{\"id\":" << entry.id.node() << ",\"type\":";
    write_json_string(out, node.type_name());
    out << ",\"in_plan\":" << in_plan << ",\"paused\":" << entry.paused << ",\"inputs\":";
    write_json_ports(out, node.inputs());
    out << ",\"outputs\":";
    write_json_ports(out, node.outputs());
    if (const auto *capture = dynamic_cast<const Capture *>(&node))
        write_json_queue(out, *capture, "underrun", capture->underrun_frames());
    else if (const auto *playback = dynamic_cast<const Playback *>(&node))
        write_json_queue(out, *playback, "overflow", playback->overflow_frames());
    else if (const auto *matrix = dynamic_cast<const Matrix *>(&node))
        out << ",\"gain\":" << matrix->gain();
    else if (const auto *mixer = dynamic_cast<const Mixer *>(&node))
    {
        out << ",\"slots\":[";
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < mixer->inputs().size(); ++i)
        {
            if (i)
                out << ',';
            out << "{\"high\":" << mixer->high_priority(i) << ",\"muted\":" << mixer->muted(i)
                << ",\"suppressed\":" << mixer->suppressed(i) << '}';
        }
        out << ']';
    }
    if (entry.device.state)
    {
        out << ",\"device_status\":{\"state\":";
        write_json_string(out, entry.device.state);
        out << ",\"error\":";
        write_json_string(out, error_code_name(entry.device.error.code()));
        out << '}';
    }
    out << '}';
}

void write_json_backend(std::ostream &out, const Backend &backend, NodeId endpoint)
{
    const auto *active = dynamic_cast<const ActiveBackend *>(&backend);
    using State = ActiveBackend::State;
    const auto state = active ? active->state() : (backend.running() ? State::Running : State::Stopped);
    const auto async = active && active->is_async();
    const auto error = backend.last_error();
    out << "{\"type\":";
    write_json_string(out, backend.type_name());
    out << ",\"node\":";
    if (endpoint)
        out << endpoint.node();
    else
        out << "null";
    out << ",\"state\":";
    write_json_string(out, state_name(state));
    out << ",\"error\":";
    write_json_string(out, error_code_name(error.code()));
    out << ",\"error_message\":";
    write_json_string(out, error.what());
    out << ",\"device\":";
    write_json_spec(out, backend.device_spec());
    out << ",\"max_frames\":" << backend.device_max_frames() << ",\"fir\":" << backend.uses_resampler()
        << ",\"async\":" << async << ",\"active\":" << (active != nullptr);
    if (async)
    {
        const auto drift = active->drift_ppm();
        if (std::isfinite(drift))
            out << ",\"drift_ppm\":" << drift;
    }
    const std::string *path = nullptr;
    if (const auto *capture = dynamic_cast<const WavCapture *>(&backend))
        path = &capture->path();
    else if (const auto *playback = dynamic_cast<const WavPlayback *>(&backend))
        path = &playback->path();
    if (path)
    {
        out << ",\"file\":";
        write_json_string(out, path->substr(path->find_last_of("/\\") + 1));
    }
#if WINDOWS_OS_ENVIRONMENT
    if (const auto *wasapi = dynamic_cast<const WasapiBackend *>(&backend))
    {
        const auto failure = wasapi->last_failure();
        if (failure.operation)
        {
            out << ",\"operation\":";
            write_json_string(out, failure.operation);
            out << ",\"hresult\":" << static_cast<std::uint32_t>(failure.hresult);
        }
    }
#endif
    out << '}';
}

struct ConfiguredTopology
{
    View<const ConfiguredNode> nodes;
    View<const Connection> connections;
};

std::string serialize_json(const Engine &engine, View<const Backend *const> backends, const ConfiguredTopology *configured)
{
    const auto plan = engine.plan_nodes();
    const auto committed_connections = engine.plan_connections();
    const auto connections = configured ? configured->connections : committed_connections;
    std::set<const Node *> plan_nodes;
    std::set<ConnId> plan_connections;
    if (configured)
        for (const auto &connection : committed_connections)
            plan_connections.insert(connection.id);

    std::ostringstream out;
    out.imbue(std::locale::classic());
    out << std::boolalpha << std::setprecision(std::numeric_limits<float>::max_digits10);
    out << "{\n  \"version\":1,\n  \"engine\":{\"state\":";
    write_json_string(out, state_name(engine.state()));
    out << ",\"period_ms\":" << static_cast<std::uint32_t>(engine.block_period())
        << ",\"plan\":" << engine.plan_generation() << ",\"tick\":" << engine.tick_position()
        << ",\"tick_us\":" << engine.last_tick_us() << ",\"missed_periods\":" << engine.missed_periods()
        << ",\"dirty\":" << engine.dirty() << "},\n  \"plan_order\":[";
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < plan.size(); ++i)
    {
        if (configured)
            plan_nodes.insert(&engine.plan_node_at(i));
        if (i)
            out << ',';
        out << plan[i].node();
    }
    out << "],\n  \"nodes\":[";

    std::map<const Node *, NodeId> endpoint_ids;
    const auto node_count = configured ? configured->nodes.size() : plan.size();
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < node_count; ++i)
    {
        const auto entry = configured ? configured->nodes[i] : ConfiguredNode{plan[i], &engine.plan_node_at(i), false};
        if (!backends.empty())
            endpoint_ids.emplace(entry.node, entry.id);
        out << (i ? ",\n    " : "\n    ");
        write_json_node(out, entry, !configured || plan_nodes.count(entry.node) != 0);
    }
    out << "\n  ],\n  \"connections\":[";
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < connections.size(); ++i)
    {
        const auto &connection = connections[i];
        out << (i ? ",\n    " : "\n    ") << "{\"id\":" << connection.id.value
            << ",\"from\":[" << connection.from.node() << ',' << connection.from.port()
            << "],\"to\":[" << connection.to.node() << ',' << connection.to.port()
            << "],\"in_plan\":" << (!configured || plan_connections.count(connection.id) != 0) << '}';
    }
    out << "\n  ],\n  \"backends\":[";
    bool first = true;
    for (const auto *backend : backends)
    {
        if (!backend)
            continue;
        out << (first ? "\n    " : ",\n    ");
        first = false;
        const auto found = endpoint_ids.find(backend->endpoint_node());
        write_json_backend(out, *backend, found == endpoint_ids.end() ? NodeId{} : found->second);
    }
    out << "\n  ]\n}\n";
    return out.str();
}

} // namespace

std::string dump_json(const Engine &engine, View<const Backend *const> backends)
{
    return serialize_json(engine, backends, nullptr);
}

std::string dump_json(const Engine &engine, View<const ConfiguredNode> nodes, View<const Connection> connections,
                      View<const Backend *const> backends)
{
    const ConfiguredTopology configured{nodes, connections};
    return serialize_json(engine, backends, &configured);
}

} // namespace aereborn

#ifndef AEREBORN_DIAG_INSPECT_H

namespace aereborn

/// @brief Return versioned JSON of the committed plan and supplied backends.
/// @pre Call on the serialized control side with live backends and endpoints.
/// @note Metrics are sampled independently. WAV backends expose only the configured file name.
std::string dump_json(const Engine &engine, View<const Backend *const> backends = {});

struct DeviceStatus
{
    const char *state = nullptr;
    RetCode error;
};

struct ConfiguredNode
{
    NodeId id;
    const Node *node;
    bool paused;
    DeviceStatus device{};
};

/// @brief Return JSON of the caller's configured topology, marking members of the committed plan.
/// @pre Call on the serialized control side with live node and backend pointers.
std::string dump_json(const Engine &engine, View<const ConfiguredNode> nodes, View<const Connection> connections,
                      View<const Backend *const> backends);

} // namespace aereborn

===== app/audio_center.cpp =====
#include "audio_interface.h"
#include "backend/pull_sink.h"
#include "backend/push_source.h"
#include "backend/usr_capture.h"
#include "backend/usr_playback.h"
#include "backend/wav_backend.h"
#include "core/command_queue.h"
#include "device_service.h"
#include "diag/inspect.h"
#include "graph_service.h"
#include <functional>
#include <limits>
#include <mutex>
#include <new>
#include <utility>

namespace aereborn
{
namespace
{

thread_local RetCode thread_last_error;

template <class Fn> auto invoke(Fn &&fn) noexcept -> decltype(fn())
{
    try
    {
        return fn();
    }
    catch (const Error &error)
    {
        thread_last_error = error.code();
    }
    catch (const std::bad_alloc &)
    {
        thread_last_error = RetCode::TOOLARGE;
    }
    catch (...)
    {
        thread_last_error = RetCode::FAILED;
    }
    return decltype(fn())();
}

template <class Fn> auto dispatch(CommandQueue &commands, Fn &&fn) noexcept -> decltype(fn())
{
    thread_last_error = RetCode::OK;
    return invoke([&] { return commands.sync(fn); });
}

template <class Fn> RetCode call_status(CommandQueue &commands, Fn &&fn) noexcept
{
    dispatch(commands, fn);
    return thread_last_error;
}

template <class Driver>
struct ActiveBinding final : NodeBinding
{
    template <class... Args>
    explicit ActiveBinding(Args &&...args) : driver(std::forward<Args>(args)...) {}

    const Backend *backend() const noexcept override { return &driver; }
    void start() override { driver.start(); }
    void stop() noexcept override { driver.stop(); }
    void unbind() noexcept override { driver.unbind(); }

    Driver driver;
};

template <class Driver>
struct PassiveBinding final : NodeBinding
{
    // Constructing the driver validates the PCM spec before the frame limit is calculated.
    explicit PassiveBinding(PortSpec spec)
        : driver(spec), max_frames_(std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / spec.channels / sample_bytes(spec.format))
    {
    }

    const Backend *backend() const noexcept override { return &driver; }

    void start() override
    {
        const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        driver.start();
    }

    void stop() noexcept override
    {
        const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        driver.stop();
    }

    void unbind() noexcept override
    {
        const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        driver.unbind();
    }

    Driver driver;

    template <class Byte, class Block>
    Result<std::size_t> transfer(Byte *data, std::size_t frames, std::size_t (Driver::*operation)(Block))
    {
        if (frames && !data)
            return RetCode::EPARAM;
        if (frames > max_frames_)
            return RetCode::TOOLARGE;

        const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
        if (!driver.running())
            return driver.bound() ? RetCode::ESTATE : RetCode::ENODE;
        return (driver.*operation)({data, driver.device_spec(), frames});
    }

  private:
    std::mutex mutex_;
    const std::size_t max_frames_;
};

template <class Endpoint, class Binding>
NodeId add_endpoint(GraphService &graph, std::unique_ptr<Endpoint> endpoint, std::shared_ptr<Binding> binding)
{
    auto *node = endpoint.get();
    auto *endpoint_binding = binding.get();
    return graph.add_endpoint(std::move(endpoint), std::move(binding),
                              [endpoint_binding, node] { endpoint_binding->driver.bind(*node); });
}

template <class Endpoint, class Binding>
NodeId add_pcm_endpoint(GraphService &graph, PortSpec spec, std::chrono::microseconds io_period,
                        std::shared_ptr<Binding> binding)
{
    // The binding validates bandwidth. Reject sub-frame periods before allocating the queue.
    constexpr auto micros_per_second = 1000000u;
    const auto rate = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(spec.bandwidth);
    const std::chrono::microseconds min_period{(micros_per_second + rate - 1) / rate};
    if (io_period < min_period)
        throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM);
    auto endpoint = std::make_unique<Endpoint>(spec.bandwidth, spec.channels, io_period);
    return add_endpoint(graph, std::move(endpoint), std::move(binding));
}

template <class Binding> bool matches(const NodeBinding &binding) noexcept
{
    return dynamic_cast<const Binding *>(&binding) != nullptr;
}

template <class Driver, class Byte, class Block>
std::size_t transfer_pcm(GraphService &graph, NodeId node, Byte *data, std::size_t frames,
                         std::size_t (Driver::*operation)(Block)) noexcept
{
    using Binding = PassiveBinding<Driver>;
    return invoke([&] {
        const auto endpoint = graph.find_binding(node, matches<Binding>);
        const auto result = endpoint ? static_cast<Binding &>(*endpoint.value()).transfer(data, frames, operation)
                                     : Result<std::size_t>(endpoint.ret_code());
        thread_last_error = result.ret_code();
        return result ? result.value() : 0;
    });
}

} // namespace

AudioCenter::AudioCenter(BlockPeriod period)
    : commands_(std::make_unique<CommandQueue>())
{
    commands_->sync([&] {
        auto graph = std::make_unique<GraphService>(*commands_, period);
        auto devices = std::make_unique<DeviceService>(*commands_, *graph);
        graph_ = std::move(graph);
        devices_ = std::move(devices);
    });
}

AudioCenter::~AudioCenter()
{
    const auto shutdown = [this]() noexcept {
        devices_->cancel_poll();
        graph_.reset();
        devices_.reset();
    };
    commands_->stop(std::cref(shutdown)); // stop() joins before the borrowed callable goes out of scope.
}

NodeId AudioCenter::create_idev(const DevConfig &config, DevFlags flags)
{
    return dispatch(*commands_, [&] { return devices_->create_idev(config, flags); });
}

NodeId AudioCenter::create_odev(const DevConfig &config, DevFlags flags)
{
    return dispatch(*commands_, [&] { return devices_->create_odev(config, flags); });
}

NodeId AudioCenter::create_iwav(const std::string &path, int repeat, std::chrono::microseconds io_period)
{
    return dispatch(*commands_, [&] {
        using Binding = ActiveBinding<WavCapture>;
        auto binding = repeat > 0 ? std::make_shared<Binding>(path, WavCapture::Repeat{static_cast<std::size_t>(repeat)})
                                  : std::make_shared<Binding>(path, repeat < 0);
        const auto spec = binding->driver.probe().value_or_throw();
        auto endpoint = std::make_unique<Capture>(spec.bandwidth, spec.channels, io_period);
        return add_endpoint(*graph_, std::move(endpoint), std::move(binding));
    });
}

NodeId AudioCenter::create_owav(const std::string &path, PortSpec spec, std::chrono::seconds segment_duration,
                               std::chrono::microseconds io_period)
{
    return dispatch(*commands_, [&] {
        if (!spec.valid())
            throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM);
        throw_if_error(WavFile::max_write_frames(spec).ret_code());
        auto endpoint = std::make_unique<Playback>(spec.bandwidth, spec.channels, io_period);
        auto binding = std::make_shared<ActiveBinding<WavPlayback>>(path, spec.format, segment_duration,
                                                                   WavFile::WriteMode::Exclusive);
        return add_endpoint(*graph_, std::move(endpoint), std::move(binding));
    });
}

NodeId AudioCenter::create_icallback(PortSpec spec, AudioCallback callback, void *user_data,
                                    std::chrono::microseconds io_period)
{
    return dispatch(*commands_, [&] {
        if (!callback)
            throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM);
        auto binding = std::make_shared<ActiveBinding<UsrCapture>>(spec, [callback, user_data](OBBlock block) noexcept {
            callback(nullptr, block.byte_view().data(), block.frames(), block.spec(), user_data);
        });
        return add_pcm_endpoint<Capture>(*graph_, spec, io_period, std::move(binding));
    });
}

NodeId AudioCenter::create_ocallback(PortSpec spec, AudioCallback callback, void *user_data,
                                    std::chrono::microseconds io_period)
{
    return dispatch(*commands_, [&] {
        if (!callback)
            throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM);
        auto binding = std::make_shared<ActiveBinding<UsrPlayback>>(spec, [callback, user_data](IBBlock block) noexcept {
            callback(block.byte_view().data(), nullptr, block.frames(), block.spec(), user_data);
        });
        return add_pcm_endpoint<Playback>(*graph_, spec, io_period, std::move(binding));
    });
}

NodeId AudioCenter::create_ipush(PortSpec spec, std::chrono::microseconds io_period)
{
    return dispatch(*commands_, [&] {
        auto binding = std::make_shared<PassiveBinding<PushSource>>(spec);
        return add_pcm_endpoint<Capture>(*graph_, spec, io_period, std::move(binding));
    });
}

NodeId AudioCenter::create_opull(PortSpec spec, std::chrono::microseconds io_period)
{
    return dispatch(*commands_, [&] {
        auto binding = std::make_shared<PassiveBinding<PullSink>>(spec);
        return add_pcm_endpoint<Playback>(*graph_, spec, io_period, std::move(binding));
    });
}

std::size_t AudioCenter::push(NodeId node, const void *data, std::size_t frames) noexcept
{
    return transfer_pcm(*graph_, node, static_cast<const std::uint8_t *>(data), frames, &PushSource::push);
}

std::size_t AudioCenter::pull(NodeId node, void *data, std::size_t frames) noexcept
{
    return transfer_pcm(*graph_, node, static_cast<std::uint8_t *>(data), frames, &PullSink::pull);
}

NodeId AudioCenter::create_mixer(BandWidth bandwidth, std::size_t channels, std::size_t inputs)
{
    return dispatch(*commands_, [&] { return graph_->create_mixer(bandwidth, channels, inputs); });
}

NodeId AudioCenter::create_matrix(BandWidth bandwidth, const ChannelMap &map, float gain)
{
    return dispatch(*commands_, [&] {
        throw_if_error(map.status());
        const auto &coefficients = map.coefficients();
        return graph_->create_matrix(bandwidth, map.input_channels(), map.output_channels(),
                                     {coefficients.data(), coefficients.size()}, gain);
    });
}

NodeId AudioCenter::create_convert(PortSpec input, PortSpec output)
{
    return dispatch(*commands_, [&] { return graph_->create_convert(input, output); });
}

PortSpec AudioCenter::output_spec(NodeId port) const
{
    return dispatch(*commands_, [&] { return graph_->port_spec(port, true); });
}

    return call_status(*commands_, [&] { graph_->connect(from, to); });
}

{
    return call_status(*commands_, [&] { graph_->disconnect(from, to); });

    return call_status(*commands_, [&] { graph_->disconnect(to); });
}

RetCode AudioCenter::remove(NodeId node)
{
    return call_status(*commands_, [&] { graph_->remove(node); });
}

RetCode AudioCenter::commit()
{
    return call_status(*commands_, [&] { graph_->commit(); });
}

RetCode AudioCenter::start()
{
    return call_status(*commands_, [&] { graph_->start(); });
}

RetCode AudioCenter::stop() noexcept
{
    return call_status(*commands_, [&] { graph_->stop(); });
}

RetCode AudioCenter::set_paused(NodeId node, bool paused)
{
    return call_status(*commands_, [&] { graph_->set_paused(node, paused); });
}

RetCode AudioCenter::set_gain(NodeId matrix, float gain)
{
    return call_status(*commands_, [&] { graph_->set_gain(matrix, gain); });

    return call_status(*commands_, [&] { graph_->set_muted(mixer_port, muted); });

    return call_status(*commands_, [&] { graph_->set_high_priority(mixer_port, high); });
}

std::string AudioCenter::dump_json() const
{
    return dispatch(*commands_, [&] {
        return graph_->dump_json([this](NodeId id) noexcept { return devices_->status_of(id); });
    });
}

RetCode AudioCenter::last_error() noexcept

}

===== app/channel_map.cpp =====
#include "audio_interface.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <new>

namespace aereborn
{
namespace
{

RetCode check_dimensions(std::size_t inputs, std::size_t outputs, std::size_t capacity) noexcept
{
    if (!inputs || !outputs)
        return RetCode::EPARAM;
    if (outputs > capacity / inputs)
        return RetCode::TOOLARGE;
    return RetCode::OK;
}

} // namespace

ChannelMap::ChannelMap(std::size_t input_channels, std::size_t output_channels) noexcept
    : status_(check_dimensions(input_channels, output_channels, coefficients_.max_size()))
{
    if (!status_)
        return;

    try
    {
        coefficients_.resize(input_channels * output_channels);
        input_channels_ = input_channels;
        output_channels_ = output_channels;
    }
    catch (const std::bad_alloc &)
    {
        status_ = RetCode::TOOLARGE;
    }
}

ChannelMap ChannelMap::identity(std::size_t channels) noexcept
{
    ChannelMap map(channels, channels);
    if (map.valid())
        for (std::size_t channel = 0; channel < channels; ++channel)
            map.coefficients_[channel * channels + channel] = 1.0f;
    return map;
}

ChannelMap ChannelMap::select(std::size_t input_channels, const std::vector<std::size_t> &sources) noexcept
{
    return select(input_channels, sources.data(), sources.size());
}

ChannelMap ChannelMap::select(std::size_t input_channels, std::initializer_list<std::size_t> sources) noexcept
{
    return select(input_channels, sources.begin(), sources.size());
}

ChannelMap ChannelMap::select(std::size_t input_channels, const std::size_t *sources, std::size_t count) noexcept
{
    for (std::size_t output = 0; output < count; ++output)
        if (sources[output] >= input_channels)
            return {};

    ChannelMap map(input_channels, count);
    if (map.valid())
        for (std::size_t output = 0; output < count; ++output)
            map.coefficients_[output * input_channels + sources[output]] = 1.0f;
    return map;
}

ChannelMap ChannelMap::slice(std::size_t input_channels, std::size_t first, std::size_t count) noexcept
{
    if (first > input_channels || count > input_channels - first)
        return {};

    ChannelMap map(input_channels, count);
    if (map.valid())
        for (std::size_t output = 0; output < count; ++output)
            map.coefficients_[output * input_channels + first + output] = 1.0f;
    return map;
}

ChannelMap ChannelMap::to_mono(std::size_t input_channels) noexcept
{
    ChannelMap map(input_channels, 1);
    if (map.valid())
        std::fill(map.coefficients_.begin(), map.coefficients_.end(), 1.0f / static_cast<float>(input_channels));
    return map;
}

ChannelMap ChannelMap::from_mono(std::size_t output_channels) noexcept
{
    ChannelMap map(1, output_channels);
    std::fill(map.coefficients_.begin(), map.coefficients_.end(), 1.0f);
    return map;
}

ChannelMap ChannelMap::dense(std::size_t input_channels, std::size_t output_channels,
                             const std::vector<float> &coefficients) noexcept
{
    return dense(input_channels, output_channels, coefficients.data(), coefficients.size());
}

ChannelMap ChannelMap::dense(std::size_t input_channels, std::size_t output_channels,
                             std::initializer_list<float> coefficients) noexcept
{
    return dense(input_channels, output_channels, coefficients.begin(), coefficients.size());
}

ChannelMap ChannelMap::dense(std::size_t input_channels, std::size_t output_channels, const float *coefficients,
                             std::size_t count) noexcept
{
    ChannelMap map;
    map.status_ = check_dimensions(input_channels, output_channels, map.coefficients_.max_size());
    if (!map.status_)
        return map;
    if (count != input_channels * output_channels ||
        !std::all_of(coefficients, coefficients + count, [](float weight) { return std::isfinite(weight); }))
        return {};

    try
    {
        map.coefficients_.assign(coefficients, coefficients + count);
        map.input_channels_ = input_channels;
        map.output_channels_ = output_channels;
    }
    catch (const std::bad_alloc &)
    {
        map.status_ = RetCode::TOOLARGE;
    }
    return map;
}

ChannelMap &ChannelMap::route(std::size_t from, std::size_t to, float weight) noexcept
{
    if (auto *row = output_row(to))
    {
        if (from < input_channels_ && std::isfinite(weight))
            row[from] = weight;
        else
            status_ = RetCode::EPARAM;
    }
    return *this;
}

ChannelMap &ChannelMap::mix(const std::vector<std::size_t> &from, std::size_t to, float gain) noexcept
{
    return mix(from.data(), from.size(), to, gain);
}

ChannelMap &ChannelMap::mix(std::initializer_list<std::size_t> from, std::size_t to, float gain) noexcept
{
    return mix(from.begin(), from.size(), to, gain);
}

ChannelMap &ChannelMap::mix(const std::size_t *from, std::size_t count, std::size_t to, float gain) noexcept
{
    auto *row = output_row(to);
    if (!row)
        return *this;
    if (!count || count > input_channels_ || !std::isfinite(gain))
    {
        status_ = RetCode::EPARAM;
        return *this;
    }
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i)
    {
        if (from[i] >= input_channels_ || std::find(from, from + i, from[i]) != from + i)
        {
            status_ = RetCode::EPARAM;
            return *this;
        }
    }

    const auto weight = gain / static_cast<float>(count);
    std::fill_n(row, input_channels_, 0.0f);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i)
        row[from[i]] = weight;
    return *this;
}

ChannelMap &ChannelMap::scale_output(std::size_t to, float factor) noexcept
{
    auto *row = output_row(to);
    if (!row)
        return *this;
    const auto end = row + input_channels_;
    if (!std::isfinite(factor) ||
        !std::all_of(row, end, [factor](float weight) { return std::isfinite(weight * factor); }))
    {
        status_ = RetCode::EPARAM;
        return *this;
    }
    for (auto *weight = row; weight != end; ++weight)
        *weight *= factor;
    return *this;
}

bool ChannelMap::valid() const noexcept
{
    return static_cast<bool>(status());
}

RetCode ChannelMap::status() const noexcept
{
    if (status_ && (!input_channels_ || !output_channels_ || coefficients_.size() != input_channels_ * output_channels_))
        return RetCode::EPARAM;
    return status_;
}

float ChannelMap::weight(std::size_t from, std::size_t to) const noexcept
{
    if (!valid() || from >= input_channels_ || to >= output_channels_)
        return 0.0f;
    return coefficients_[to * input_channels_ + from];
}

float *ChannelMap::output_row(std::size_t to) noexcept
{
    if (!valid())
        return nullptr;
    if (to >= output_channels_)
    {
        status_ = RetCode::EPARAM;
        return nullptr;
    }
    return coefficients_.data() + to * input_channels_;
}

} // namespace aereborn

===== app/device_service.cpp =====
#include "device_service.h"
#include "audio_interface.h"
#include "diag/inspect.h"
#include "graph_service.h"
#include "plugins/endpoint.h"
#if defined(AEREBORN_HAS_ALSA)
#include "backend/alsa_backend.h"
#elif WINDOWS_OS_ENVIRONMENT
#include "backend/wasapi_backend.h"
#endif
#include <algorithm>
#include <cassert>
#include <new>
#include <utility>

namespace aereborn
{
namespace
{

constexpr auto recovery_flags = DevFlags::RECOVERSOFT | DevFlags::RECOVERHARD;
#if defined(AEREBORN_HAS_ALSA) || WINDOWS_OS_ENVIRONMENT
constexpr auto device_health_interval = std::chrono::seconds(1);
constexpr auto device_reset_interval = std::chrono::seconds(10); // Shortened during device recovery testing.
#endif

} // namespace

#if defined(AEREBORN_HAS_ALSA) || WINDOWS_OS_ENVIRONMENT
struct DeviceService::DeviceBinding final : NodeBinding
{
    using Clock = CommandQueue::Clock;
    enum class Status { Stopped, Online, Waiting, Failed };

    DeviceBinding(const DevConfig &config, DevFlags flags) : config_(config), flags_(flags) {}
    ~DeviceBinding() override { unbind(); }

    void attach(DeviceService &service, NodeId id) noexcept
    {
        service_ = &service;
        id_ = id;
    }

    void bind(Capture &node)
    {
        capture_ = &node;
        bind_initial();
    }

    void bind(Playback &node)
    {
        playback_ = &node;
        bind_initial();
    }

    const Backend *backend() const noexcept override { return driver_.get(); }

    DeviceStatus device_status() const noexcept
    {
        auto status = status_;
        auto error = last_error_;
        if (status == Status::Online && !driver_->running())
        {
            error = worker_error();
            status = failure_status(error);
        }
        constexpr const char *names[] = {"stopped", "online", "waiting", "failed"};
        return {names[static_cast<unsigned>(status)], error};
    }

    void start() override
    {
        start_driver();
        if (needs_poll())
            service_->on_device_started();
    }

    void stop() noexcept override
    {
        const auto was_polled = needs_poll();
        status_ = Status::Stopped;
        if (driver_)
        {
            driver_->stop();
            const auto error = driver_->last_error();
            if (!error)
                last_error_ = error;
            if (!error || config_.name.empty() || config_.name == "default")
                driver_.reset();
        }
        if (was_polled)
            service_->on_device_stopped();
    }

    void unbind() noexcept override
    {
        if (service_)
        {
            service_->unregister(id_);
            service_ = nullptr;
        }
        driver_.reset();
        status_ = Status::Stopped;
    }

    bool needs_poll() const noexcept
    {
        return recovers() && (status_ == Status::Online || status_ == Status::Waiting);
    }

    bool poll(Clock::time_point now) noexcept
    {
        if (!needs_poll())
            return false;
        if (status_ == Status::Online)
        {
            if (!driver_->running())
            {
                fail(worker_error());
                if (status_ == Status::Failed)
                    return false;
            }
            else if (!strict() || now < reset_at_)
                return true;
            else
                driver_.reset();
        }
        start_driver();
        return needs_poll();
    }

  private:
    bool recovers() const noexcept { return (flags_ & recovery_flags) != DevFlags::NONE; }
    bool strict() const noexcept { return has_flag(flags_, DevFlags::RECOVERHARD); }

    static bool device_unavailable(RetCode code) noexcept
    {
        return code == RetCode::DEVLOST || code == RetCode::IOFAILED;
    }

    void bind_initial()
    {
        try
        {
            open_driver();
        }
        catch (const Error &error)
        {
            if (!recovers() || !device_unavailable(error.code()))
                throw;
            last_error_ = error.code();
        }
    }

    void open_driver()
    {
        const auto fail_on_timeout = !has_flag(flags_, DevFlags::RECOVERSOFT);
#if defined(AEREBORN_HAS_ALSA)
        auto driver = std::make_unique<AlsaBackend>(config_.name, config_.device, !has_flag(flags_, DevFlags::MEMCPY),
                                                    fail_on_timeout);
#else
        auto driver = std::make_unique<WasapiBackend>(config_.name, fail_on_timeout);
#endif
        if (capture_)
            driver->bind(*capture_);
        else
            driver->bind(*playback_);
        driver_ = std::move(driver);
    }

    void start_driver()
    {
        try
        {
            if (!driver_)
                open_driver();
            driver_->start();
            if (strict())
                reset_at_ = Clock::now() + device_reset_interval;
            last_error_ = {};
            status_ = Status::Online;
        }
        catch (const Error &error)
        {
            fail(error.code());
            if (!recovers())
                throw;
        }
        catch (const std::bad_alloc &)
        {
            fail(RetCode::TOOLARGE);
            if (!recovers())
                throw;
        }
        catch (...)
        {
            fail(RetCode::FAILED);
            if (!recovers())
                throw;
        }
    }

    RetCode worker_error() const noexcept
    {
        const auto error = driver_->last_error();
        return error ? RetCode::IOFAILED : error.code();
    }

    Status failure_status(RetCode error) const noexcept
    {
        return recovers() && (device_unavailable(error) || (strict() && error == RetCode::TIMEOUT))
                   ? Status::Waiting : Status::Failed;
    }

    void fail(RetCode error) noexcept
    {
        last_error_ = error;
        driver_.reset();
        status_ = failure_status(error);
    }

    const DevConfig config_;
    const DevFlags flags_;
    DeviceService *service_ = nullptr;
    NodeId id_;
    Capture *capture_ = nullptr;
    Playback *playback_ = nullptr;
    std::unique_ptr<ActiveBackend> driver_;
    RetCode last_error_;
    Status status_ = Status::Stopped;
    Clock::time_point reset_at_;
};
#endif

DeviceService::DeviceService(CommandQueue &commands, GraphService &graph) : commands_(commands), graph_(graph)
{
}

DeviceService::~DeviceService()
{
    assert(devices_.empty());
    cancel_poll();
}

template <class Endpoint> NodeId DeviceService::create_device(const DevConfig &config, DevFlags flags)
{
    assert(commands_.on_thread());
    constexpr auto known_flags = recovery_flags | DevFlags::MEMCPY;
    if (!is_block_period(config.period) || config.name.find('\0') != std::string::npos ||
        (flags & known_flags) != flags || has_flag(flags, recovery_flags))
        throw Error(RetCode::EPARAM);

    const std::chrono::milliseconds period(static_cast<std::uint32_t>(config.period));
    auto endpoint = std::make_unique<Endpoint>(config.bandwidth, config.channels, period);
#if defined(AEREBORN_HAS_ALSA) || WINDOWS_OS_ENVIRONMENT
    auto binding = std::make_shared<DeviceBinding>(config, flags);
    auto *node = endpoint.get();
    auto *device = binding.get();
    const auto id = graph_.add_endpoint(std::move(endpoint), std::move(binding), [device, node] { device->bind(*node); });
    const auto cleanup = [&]() noexcept { graph_.remove(id); };
    ScopeExit<decltype(cleanup)> rollback(cleanup);
    devices_.emplace(id, device);
    device->attach(*this, id);
    rollback.dismiss();
    return id;
#else
    throw Error(RetCode::IOFAILED);
#endif
}

NodeId DeviceService::create_idev(const DevConfig &config, DevFlags flags)
{
    return create_device<Capture>(config, flags);
}

NodeId DeviceService::create_odev(const DevConfig &config, DevFlags flags)
{
    return create_device<Playback>(config, flags);
}

void DeviceService::cancel_poll() noexcept
{
    commands_.cancel(poll_timer_);
    poll_timer_ = 0;
}

#if defined(AEREBORN_HAS_ALSA) || WINDOWS_OS_ENVIRONMENT
DeviceStatus DeviceService::status_of(NodeId node) const noexcept
{
    const auto device = devices_.find(node);
    return device == devices_.end() ? DeviceStatus{} : device->second->device_status();
}

void DeviceService::on_device_started()
{
    if (!poll_timer_)
        poll_timer_ = commands_.post_every(device_health_interval, [this] { poll_devices(); });
}

void DeviceService::on_device_stopped() noexcept
{
    if (poll_timer_ && std::none_of(devices_.begin(), devices_.end(), [](const auto &entry) {
            return entry.second->needs_poll();
        }))
        cancel_poll();
}

void DeviceService::unregister(NodeId id) noexcept
{
    const auto entry = devices_.find(id);
    const auto was_polled = entry->second->needs_poll();
    devices_.erase(entry);
    if (was_polled)
        on_device_stopped();
}

void DeviceService::poll_devices() noexcept
{
    const auto now = CommandQueue::Clock::now();
    bool required = false;
    for (const auto &entry : devices_)
        required |= entry.second->poll(now);
    if (!required)
        cancel_poll();
}
#else
DeviceStatus DeviceService::status_of(NodeId) const noexcept
{
    return {};
}
#endif

} // namespace aereborn

===== app/device_service.h =====
#ifndef AEREBORN_APP_DEVICE_SERVICE_H
#define AEREBORN_APP_DEVICE_SERVICE_H

#include "core/command_queue.h"
#include <map>

namespace aereborn
{

class GraphService;
struct DeviceStatus;
struct DevConfig;
enum class DevFlags : std::uint32_t;

/// @brief Manage hardware binding recovery on the graph's control thread.
/// @note Destroy the graph before the service so all bindings can unregister.
class DeviceService
{
  public:
    DeviceService(CommandQueue &commands, GraphService &graph);
    ~DeviceService();

    NodeId create_idev(const DevConfig &config, DevFlags flags);
    NodeId create_odev(const DevConfig &config, DevFlags flags);

    /// @brief Query hardware status or return an empty status for other nodes.
    DeviceStatus status_of(NodeId node) const noexcept;
    /// @brief Cancel the shared timer before graph teardown.
    void cancel_poll() noexcept;

  private:
    struct DeviceBinding;
    template <class Endpoint> NodeId create_device(const DevConfig &config, DevFlags flags);

    void on_device_started();
    void on_device_stopped() noexcept;
    void unregister(NodeId id) noexcept;
    void poll_devices() noexcept;

    CommandQueue &commands_;
    GraphService &graph_;
    CommandQueue::TimerId poll_timer_ = 0;
    std::map<NodeId, DeviceBinding *> devices_;
};

} // namespace aereborn

#endif // AEREBORN_APP_DEVICE_SERVICE_H

===== app/graph_service.cpp =====
#include "graph_service.h"
#include "core/command_queue.h"
#include "diag/inspect.h"
#include "plugins/convert.h"
#include "plugins/matrix.h"
#include "plugins/mixer.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cassert>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

namespace aereborn
{
namespace
{

bool contains_node(View<const NodeId> nodes, NodeId id) noexcept
{
    return !nodes.empty() && std::find(nodes.begin(), nodes.end(), id) != nodes.end();
}

} // namespace

GraphService::GraphService(CommandQueue &commands, BlockPeriod period)
    : commands_(commands), engine_(period)
{
}

GraphService::~GraphService()
{
    assert(commands_.on_thread());
    // External calls have already returned, so teardown can unbind directly.
    for (auto it = nodes_.rbegin(); it != nodes_.rend(); ++it)
        if (const auto &endpoint = it->second.endpoint)
            endpoint->unbind();
    engine_.stop();
}

NodeId GraphService::add_node(std::unique_ptr<Node> node)
{
    assert(commands_.on_thread());
    return insert_node(std::move(node), /*published=*/true)->first;
}

GraphService::NodeMap::iterator GraphService::insert_node(std::unique_ptr<Node> node, bool published)
{
    const auto id = engine_.add_node(std::move(node));
    const auto cleanup = [&]() noexcept { engine_.remove_node(id); };
    ScopeExit<decltype(cleanup)> rollback(cleanup);
    const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(nodes_mutex_);
    const auto entry = nodes_.emplace(id, NodeEntry{{}, published}).first;
    rollback.dismiss();
    return entry;
}

std::shared_ptr<NodeBinding> GraphService::detach_node(NodeMap::iterator entry)
{
    const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(nodes_mutex_);
    auto endpoint = std::move(entry->second.endpoint);
    nodes_.erase(entry);
    return endpoint;
}

NodeId GraphService::add_endpoint(std::unique_ptr<Node> endpoint, std::shared_ptr<NodeBinding> binding,
                                 const std::function<void()> &bind)
{
    assert(commands_.on_thread());
    endpoint->prepare(engine_.block_period());
    // Reserve the table entry before bind() can create a file, then publish after binding.
    const auto entry = insert_node(std::move(endpoint), /*published=*/false);
    const auto id = entry->first;
    const auto cleanup = [&]() noexcept {
        binding.reset(); // Destroy the unpublished backend before removing the node it borrows.
        detach_node(entry);
        engine_.remove_node(id);
    };
    ScopeExit<decltype(cleanup)> rollback(cleanup);
    bind();
    {
        const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(nodes_mutex_);
        entry->second.endpoint = std::move(binding);
        entry->second.published = true;
    }
    rollback.dismiss();
    return id;
}

Result<std::shared_ptr<NodeBinding>> GraphService::find_binding(NodeId node, BindingMatcher matches) noexcept
{
    if (node.port() != 0)
        return RetCode::EPORT;

    const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(nodes_mutex_);
    const auto entry = nodes_.find(node);
    if (entry == nodes_.end() || !entry->second.published)
        return RetCode::ENODE;
    const auto &endpoint = entry->second.endpoint;
    if (endpoint && matches(*endpoint))
        return endpoint;
    return RetCode::ETYPE;
}

NodeId GraphService::create_mixer(BandWidth bandwidth, std::size_t channels, std::size_t inputs)
{
    return add_node(std::make_unique<Mixer>(bandwidth, channels, inputs));
}

NodeId GraphService::create_matrix(BandWidth bandwidth, std::size_t input_channels, std::size_t output_channels,
                                   View<const float> coefficients, float gain)
{
    return add_node(std::make_unique<Matrix>(bandwidth, input_channels, output_channels, coefficients, gain));
}

NodeId GraphService::create_convert(PortSpec input, PortSpec output)
{
    return add_node(std::make_unique<Convert>(input, output));
}

GraphService::NodeMap::iterator GraphService::find_node(NodeId node)
{
    assert(commands_.on_thread());
    if (node.port() != 0)
        throw Error(RetCode::EPORT);
    const auto entry = nodes_.find(node);
    if (entry == nodes_.end())
        throw Error(RetCode::ENODE);
    return entry;
}

PortSpec GraphService::port_spec(NodeId port, bool output)
{
    find_node(port.base());
    const auto *node = engine_.node(port.base());
    const auto ports = output ? node->outputs() : node->inputs();
    if (port.port() >= ports.size())
        throw Error(RetCode::EPORT);
    return ports[port.port()];
}

void GraphService::connect(NodeId from, NodeId to)
{
    assert(commands_.on_thread());
    // Reserve bookkeeping before editing the draft, so an allocation failure leaves it untouched.
    const auto found = links_.lower_bound(to);
    const bool insert = found == links_.end() || found->first != to;
    const auto entry = insert ? links_.emplace_hint(found, to, Connection{}) : found;
    const auto cleanup = [&]() noexcept {
        if (insert)
            links_.erase(entry);
    };
    ScopeExit<decltype(cleanup)> rollback(cleanup);
    entry->second = {engine_.connect(from, to), from, to};
    rollback.dismiss();
}

void GraphService::disconnect(NodeId from, NodeId to)
{
    disconnect_input(to, &from);
}

void GraphService::disconnect(NodeId to)
{
    disconnect_input(to, nullptr);
}

void GraphService::disconnect_input(NodeId to, const NodeId *from)
{
    assert(commands_.on_thread());
    const auto link = links_.find(to);
    if (link == links_.end() || (from && link->second.from != *from))
    {
        if (from)
            port_spec(*from, true);
        port_spec(to, false);
        throw Error(RetCode::ECONN);
    }
    engine_.disconnect(link->second.id);
    links_.erase(link);
}

void GraphService::remove(NodeId node)
{
    if (const auto endpoint = detach_node(find_node(node)))
        endpoint->unbind();
    for (auto it = links_.begin(); it != links_.end();)
    {
        if (it->first.base() == node || it->second.from.base() == node)
            it = links_.erase(it);
        else
            ++it;
    }
    engine_.remove_node(node);
}

NodeBinding *GraphService::find_endpoint(NodeId node) noexcept
{
    const auto entry = nodes_.find(node);
    return entry == nodes_.end() ? nullptr : entry->second.endpoint.get();
}

void GraphService::commit()
{
    assert(commands_.on_thread());
    if (!engine_.dirty())
        return;

    const auto running = engine_.state() == Engine::State::Running;
    std::vector<NodeId> previous;
    if (running)
    {
        const auto plan = engine_.plan_nodes();
        if (!plan.empty())
            previous.assign(plan.begin(), plan.end());
    }

    engine_.commit();
    if (!running)
        return;

    const auto current = engine_.plan_nodes();
    const View<const NodeId> old_plan{previous.data(), previous.size()};
    const auto cleanup = [this]() noexcept { stop(); };
    ScopeExit<decltype(cleanup)> rollback(cleanup);
    for (auto it = previous.rbegin(); it != previous.rend(); ++it)
        if (auto *endpoint = find_endpoint(*it))
            if (!contains_node(current, *it))
                endpoint->stop();
    for (const auto id : current)
        if (auto *endpoint = find_endpoint(id))
            if (!contains_node(old_plan, id))
                endpoint->start();
    rollback.dismiss();
}

void GraphService::start()
{
    assert(commands_.on_thread());
    if (engine_.state() == Engine::State::Running)
        throw Error(RetCode::BUSY);
    engine_.commit();
    const auto cleanup = [this]() noexcept { stop(); };
    ScopeExit<decltype(cleanup)> rollback(cleanup);
    for (const auto id : engine_.plan_nodes())
        if (auto *endpoint = find_endpoint(id))
            endpoint->start();
    engine_.start();
    rollback.dismiss();
}

void GraphService::stop() noexcept
{
    assert(commands_.on_thread());
    for (auto it = nodes_.rbegin(); it != nodes_.rend(); ++it)
        if (const auto &endpoint = it->second.endpoint)
            endpoint->stop();
    engine_.stop();
}

void GraphService::set_paused(NodeId node, bool paused)
{
    const auto entry = find_node(node);
    engine_.set_paused(node, paused);
    entry->second.paused = paused;
}

void GraphService::set_gain(NodeId matrix, float gain)
{
    find_node(matrix);
    engine_.node<Matrix>(matrix)->set_gain(gain);
}

void GraphService::set_muted(NodeId mixer_port, bool muted)
{
    find_node(mixer_port.base());
    engine_.node<Mixer>(mixer_port.base())->set_muted(mixer_port.port(), muted);
}

void GraphService::set_high_priority(NodeId mixer_port, bool high)
{
    find_node(mixer_port.base());
    engine_.node<Mixer>(mixer_port.base())->set_high_priority(mixer_port.port(), high);
}

std::string GraphService::dump_json(const DeviceStatusFn &device_status)
{
    assert(commands_.on_thread());
    std::vector<const Backend *> drivers;
    std::vector<ConfiguredNode> nodes;
    std::vector<Connection> connections;
    nodes.reserve(nodes_.size());
    connections.reserve(links_.size());
    for (const auto &entry : nodes_)
    {
        const auto &endpoint = entry.second.endpoint;
        nodes.push_back({entry.first, engine_.node(entry.first), entry.second.paused,
                         device_status ? device_status(entry.first) : DeviceStatus{}});
        if (const auto *backend = endpoint ? endpoint->backend() : nullptr)
            drivers.push_back(backend);
    }
    for (const auto &entry : links_)
        connections.push_back(entry.second);
    std::sort(connections.begin(), connections.end(), [](const auto &a, const auto &b) { return a.id < b.id; });
    return aereborn::dump_json(engine_, {nodes.data(), nodes.size()}, {connections.data(), connections.size()},
                              {drivers.data(), drivers.size()});
}

} // namespace aereborn

===== app/graph_service.h =====
#ifndef AEREBORN_APP_GRAPH_SERVICE_H
#define AEREBORN_APP_GRAPH_SERVICE_H

#include "core/engine.h"
#include <functional>
#include <map>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <string>

namespace aereborn
{

class Backend;
class CommandQueue;
struct DeviceStatus;

struct NodeBinding
{
    virtual ~NodeBinding() = default;
    virtual const Backend *backend() const noexcept = 0;
    virtual void start() = 0;
    virtual void stop() noexcept = 0;
    virtual void unbind() noexcept = 0;
};

/// @brief Own the graph, its public handles, and its backends on the control thread.
class GraphService
{
  public:
    using DeviceStatusFn = std::function<DeviceStatus(NodeId)>;
    using BindingMatcher = bool (*)(const NodeBinding &);

    GraphService(CommandQueue &commands, BlockPeriod period);
    /// @brief Unbind backends and stop the engine on the control thread.
    ~GraphService();

    NodeId add_node(std::unique_ptr<Node> node);
    /// @brief Prepare, bind, and publish an endpoint on the control thread.
    /// @param bind Synchronous operation borrowing the supplied endpoint and binding.
    NodeId add_endpoint(std::unique_ptr<Node> endpoint, std::shared_ptr<NodeBinding> binding,
                        const std::function<void()> &bind);
    NodeId create_mixer(BandWidth bandwidth, std::size_t channels, std::size_t inputs);
    NodeId create_matrix(BandWidth bandwidth, std::size_t input_channels, std::size_t output_channels,
                         View<const float> coefficients, float gain);
    NodeId create_convert(PortSpec input, PortSpec output);
    PortSpec port_spec(NodeId port, bool output);

    void connect(NodeId from, NodeId to);
    void disconnect(NodeId from, NodeId to);
    void disconnect(NodeId to);
    void remove(NodeId node);

    void commit();
    void start();
    void stop() noexcept;

    void set_paused(NodeId node, bool paused);
    void set_gain(NodeId matrix, float gain);
    void set_muted(NodeId mixer_port, bool muted);
    void set_high_priority(NodeId mixer_port, bool high);

    /// @brief Match an endpoint under the table lock and retain it for a caller-thread transfer.
    /// @pre matches is a non-throwing predicate.
    Result<std::shared_ptr<NodeBinding>> find_binding(NodeId node, BindingMatcher matches) noexcept;

    std::string dump_json(const DeviceStatusFn &device_status = {});

  private:
    struct NodeEntry
    {
        std::shared_ptr<NodeBinding> endpoint;
        bool published;
        bool paused = false; // Control thread only.
    };
    using NodeMap = std::map<NodeId, NodeEntry>;

    NodeMap::iterator insert_node(std::unique_ptr<Node> node, bool published);
    std::shared_ptr<NodeBinding> detach_node(NodeMap::iterator entry);
    NodeMap::iterator find_node(NodeId node);
    NodeBinding *find_endpoint(NodeId node) noexcept;
    void disconnect_input(NodeId to, const NodeId *from);

    CommandQueue &commands_;
    Engine engine_;
    std::mutex nodes_mutex_;
    NodeMap nodes_;
    std::map<NodeId, Connection> links_; // Keyed by destination input port.
};

} // namespace aereborn

#endif // AEREBORN_APP_GRAPH_SERVICE_H
